# OceanX Polar Science Portal — API Documentation

**SIH26063 — Integrated Polar Science Outreach, Knowledge Repository and Media Dissemination Portal**

This document is the **single source of truth for every backend API endpoint** — implemented or planned.

- **Base URL:** `/api/v1` (same origin as the web app). Locally: `http://localhost:3000/api/v1`.
- **Contract source:** the Zod schemas and endpoint registry in [`src/contracts/`](../src/contracts). The endpoint table, the status counts and the whole of [§4 Endpoint reference](#4-endpoint-reference) are **generated** from that registry (`npm run api:generate`), and CI fails if they are stale (`npm run api:check`). Sections 1, 3 and 5 are written by hand.
- **Machine-readable spec:** [`docs/openapi.yaml`](openapi.yaml) (OpenAPI 3.1, generated from the same source).
- **Shared types:** [`src/types/api.ts`](../src/types/api.ts) · **Client:** [`src/lib/api/`](../src/lib/api) · **Mocks:** [`src/mocks/`](../src/mocks) · **Testing guide:** [`API_TESTING.md`](API_TESTING.md) · **Frontend guide:** [`FRONTEND_HANDOFF.md`](FRONTEND_HANDOFF.md)

> **Status rule.** An endpoint is `IMPLEMENTED` only when its route handler exists **and** an automated contract test for it passes. `PLANNED` endpoints describe the agreed contract; their JSON examples are illustrative and come from the mock API. Never build against an assumption that a `PLANNED` endpoint is live — use the mock API (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) until its status changes.

## 3. Conventions

### 3.1 Response envelope

Every JSON response uses one of three shapes.

**Single resource**

```json
{ "success": true, "data": { "id": "exp_demo_01", "title": "…" } }
```

**Paginated list**

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [{ "id": "exp_demo_01" }, { "id": "exp_demo_02" }],
  "pagination": { "page": 1, "limit": 12, "total": 8, "totalPages": 1, "hasNextPage": false, "hasPreviousPage": false }
}
```

`GET /search` adds a `meta` object (`query`, `tookMs`, `facets`).

**Error** (any non-2xx status)

```json
{
  "success": false,
  "error": {
    "code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
    "message": "Request validation failed.",
    "details": { "fieldErrors": { "limit": ["Too big: expected number to be <=100"] }, "formErrors": [] },
    "requestId": "req_8f2c1b7e"
  }
}
```

TypeScript: `ApiResponse<T>`, `PaginatedResponse<T>`, `ErrorResponse`, `ApiError` in `@/types/api`.

### 3.2 Errors

Each HTTP status maps to exactly one machine-readable `error.code`. Branch on `code`, not on `message` (messages are for humans and may change).

| Status | `error.code` | Meaning | What the UI should do |
|---|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON, wrong `Content-Type`, invalid/expired token. | Treat as a bug or show the message. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | No valid session (not logged in / expired), or wrong credentials on login. | Redirect to `/login?next=<current path>`; on the login form show the message. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in but not allowed (role, ownership), or request from a disallowed `Origin`. | Show “You don't have access”. Don't retry. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Record doesn't exist, isn't published, or isn't visible to you. | Render `notFound()`. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | State conflict: duplicate email/slug/DOI, already submitted, etc. `details.reason` says which. | Show the message next to the relevant field/action. |
| 413 | `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` | Upload exceeds the limit. `details.maxSizeBytes` gives the limit. | Show the limit to the user. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Query or body failed validation. `details.fieldErrors` maps field → messages. | Show messages next to form fields. |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. `Retry-After` header and `details.retryAfterSeconds`. | Disable the action and retry after the delay. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. | Show a generic error with a retry button; log `requestId`. |
| 503 | `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | Dependency down (database, AI provider). | Show a temporary-outage message; retry later. |

Every response carries an `X-Request-Id` header (also in `error.requestId`). **Include it in bug reports.**

The client (`src/lib/api-client.ts`) throws `ApiRequestError` for every failure. Besides the codes above it can produce `NETWORK_ERROR`, `TIMEOUT`, `ABORTED` (request cancelled via `AbortSignal` — usually ignore) and `INVALID_RESPONSE`. Helpers: `isUnauthenticated`, `isForbidden`, `isNotFound`, `isValidationError`, `isAborted`, `fieldErrors`.

```ts
import { getExpedition } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";

try {
  const { data: expedition } = await getExpedition(slug);
  return <ExpeditionView expedition={expedition} />;
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error) && error.isNotFound) notFound();
  throw error; // let the nearest error.tsx boundary render a retry UI
}
```

### 3.3 Authentication & authorization

- **Mechanism:** server-side sessions in an **httpOnly cookie** named `oceanx_session` (`SameSite=Lax`, `Secure` in production, `Path=/`). `POST /auth/login` and `POST /auth/register` set it; `POST /auth/logout` clears it. Sessions are stored (hashed) in the database and can be revoked.
- **The frontend never sees or stores a token.** Do not use `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` for auth. The shared client sends `credentials: "include"`, so the browser attaches the cookie automatically.
- **Who am I?** Call `GET /auth/session` — it always returns 200 with `data.user` (`null` when logged out). Don't probe protected endpoints to detect login state.
- **Server Components** do not automatically forward the browser's cookies when they call the API. Use `withRequestCookies()` from `@/lib/api/server` for per-user data (see FRONTEND_HANDOFF §6), and render it inside `<Suspense>`.
- **CSRF:** state-changing requests (`POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE`) are rejected with `403` if the `Origin` header is present and doesn't match `APP_ORIGIN`. Same-origin browser requests pass automatically.
- **Roles** (`UserRole`):

| Role | Can |
|---|---|
| `MEMBER` | Registered public user. Download `REGISTERED` files, manage own profile, upload an avatar. |
| `RESEARCHER` | Everything MEMBER can + create content as `DRAFT`, upload files, submit for review, edit own drafts. |
| `EDITOR` | Everything RESEARCHER can + edit any content, review/approve submissions, publish directly, manage news, taxonomy, AI tools. |
| `ADMIN` | Everything + user management, audit log, deletions of categories/keywords/files. |

- **Endpoint auth levels:** `Public` (no session needed) · `Public (optional session)` (works logged out; a session unlocks owner/staff views such as drafts) · `Logged in` / role list (session required).

### 3.4 Editorial workflow & visibility

Content (expeditions, publications, datasets, media, activities) has a `status`:

```
DRAFT ──submit──▶ PENDING_REVIEW ──approve──▶ PUBLISHED ──archive──▶ ARCHIVED
  ▲                  │      │
  └──withdraw────────┘      ├──request changes──▶ CHANGES_REQUESTED ──resubmit──▶ PENDING_REVIEW
                            └──reject──▶ REJECTED
```

- **Public endpoints only ever return `PUBLISHED` records.** Detail endpoints return `404` (never `403`) for records you can't see, so drafts are never revealed.
- EDITOR/ADMIN can list other statuses with `?status=` on list endpoints and can set `status` directly via `PATCH`.
- AI-generated content has its own status (`DRAFT → PUBLISHED | REJECTED | ARCHIVED`) and always requires human review before publication.

### 3.5 Pagination

- Query: `page` (1-based, default `1`) and `limit` (default per endpoint — usually 12; media 24; max 100, search max 50).
- Response: `pagination = { page, limit, total, totalPages, hasNextPage, hasPreviousPage }`.
- A page beyond the end returns `200` with `data: []` (not an error). `total: 0` means no matches → show an empty state.
- Numbered pagination: render from `totalPages`. Infinite scroll / “Load more”: request `page + 1` while `hasNextPage`.
- Keep `page` in the URL query string; reset it to `1` whenever a filter or sort changes.

### 3.6 Filtering, sorting & query parameters

- Filters are optional and combine with **AND**. Unknown query parameters are ignored.
- **Types on the wire:** numbers as decimal strings (`?year=2024`), booleans only as `true`/`false` (`?featured=true`), arrays as comma-separated lists (`?types=PUBLICATION,DATASET`). The client serializes these for you from typed values; empty strings are treated as “not set”.
- **Enums are case-sensitive and UPPER_CASE** (`region=ANTARCTICA`). The enum values are listed per endpoint and exported as TypeScript union types.
- **Taxonomy filters use slugs:** `category=glaciology`, `keyword=sea-ice`. Get them from `GET /categories` and `GET /keywords`.
- `q` performs case-insensitive text search over the fields named in each endpoint's description; use `GET /search` for cross-type search with relevance ranking and facets.
- `sort` values are listed per endpoint; `newest` is the default almost everywhere.
- Invalid values return `422 VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details.fieldErrors`.

### 3.7 File uploads & downloads

**Uploads use signed URLs, not `multipart/form-data`.** Three steps (all wrapped by `uploadFile()` in `src/lib/api/files.ts`):

1. `POST /uploads` with `{ fileName, contentType, sizeBytes, purpose }` → `UploadTicket { fileId, method: "PUT", uploadUrl, headers, expiresAt, maxSizeBytes }`. Size and type are checked here (`413` / `422`).
2. `PUT` the raw bytes to `uploadUrl` with the given `headers`. In production this is a pre-signed object-storage URL; with the local development driver it is `/api/v1/uploads/{fileId}/content`.
3. `POST /uploads/{fileId}/complete` → `FileAsset` with `status: "READY"`. Attach it by passing its `id` (e.g. `pdfFileId`, `fileIds`, `attachmentFileIds`) to a content create/update endpoint.

| Purpose | Max size | Allowed types |
|---|---|---|
| `PUBLICATION_PDF` | 50 MB | `application/pdf` |
| `DATASET_FILE` | 2 GB | NetCDF, CSV, JSON/GeoJSON, ZIP, HDF5, TIFF, text, octet-stream |
| `MEDIA` | 500 MB | JPEG/PNG/WebP/AVIF, MP4/WebM, MP3/OGG, PDF |
| `COVER_IMAGE` | 15 MB | JPEG/PNG/WebP/AVIF |
| `ATTACHMENT` | 25 MB | PDF, JPEG/PNG, text |
| `AVATAR` | 5 MB | JPEG/PNG/WebP |

(Source of truth: `UPLOAD_LIMITS` in `src/contracts/schemas/files.ts`.)

**Downloads:** every file reference (`FileRef`) has a `downloadUrl` (`/api/v1/files/{id}/download`). Render it as a plain link — `<a href={file.downloadUrl} download>` — **never `fetch()` it**. The server checks the access level (`OPEN` / `REGISTERED` → login / `RESTRICTED` → owner or staff), counts the download and redirects (`302`) to a short-lived signed URL.

### 3.8 Caching

This app runs **Next.js 16 with Cache Components** (`cacheComponents: true` in `next.config.ts`). In this model the old `fetch(url, { next: { revalidate } })` options no longer apply; caching is opt-in with `"use cache"` + `cacheLife`.

- **Server Components:** call the API at request time and wrap the component in `<Suspense>` with a skeleton fallback (Next 16 requires uncached data to be inside `<Suspense>`). Do **not** call the API inside a `"use cache"` function for now: cached functions can run during `next build`, when no API server is running, so the build would fail.
- **Client Components:** fetch on demand (e.g. in event handlers, or with a data library such as SWR/TanStack Query). Pass an `AbortSignal` so stale requests are cancelled.
- **HTTP caching (backend):** `Public` GET endpoints send `Cache-Control: public, max-age=0, s-maxage=60` (a CDN may cache for 60 s). Endpoints that read the session send `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.
- Each endpoint lists how much **staleness is acceptable**. Use that when choosing a client-side cache time (e.g. SWR `dedupingInterval`).
- Anything fetched **with a session** (drafts, admin data, `/auth/session`) must never be cached or shared between users.
- After a successful mutation, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- Never cache download URLs (signed URLs expire).

### 3.9 Loading, empty and error states

For every data-driven view, design four states:

| State | When | Guidance |
|---|---|---|
| Loading | Request in flight | Skeletons matching the final layout (e.g. `limit` skeleton cards). Use `loading.tsx` / `<Suspense>` in the App Router. |
| Empty | `data: []` / `total: 0` | Friendly message plus a “clear filters” action. Not an error. |
| Error | `ApiRequestError` thrown | Message plus retry. Use `error.tsx` boundaries for Server Components. |
| Success | Data available | — |

The mock API can force each state: `NEXT_PUBLIC_MOCK_SCENARIO=empty|error|slow|unauthenticated` (see FRONTEND_HANDOFF §3).

### 3.10 Dates, IDs, text and media

- Timestamps are ISO 8601 UTC strings (`2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`); calendar dates are `YYYY-MM-DD`. Format them in the user's locale on the client (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`).
- IDs are opaque strings — never parse or construct them. Detail endpoints accept the **slug** too, so page URLs can use slugs directly.
- Long text fields documented as *Markdown* must be rendered with a sanitising Markdown renderer. Other strings are plain text.
- Image fields include `alt` / `altText` — always use them. `width`/`height` (when present) let you reserve space and avoid layout shift.
- Placeholder/demo assets live under `/demo/…` in `public/`.

### 3.11 Rate limits

Sensitive endpoints are rate-limited (listed per endpoint). Exceeding a limit returns `429 RATE_LIMITED` with `Retry-After`. Limits are per IP for anonymous endpoints and per user for authenticated ones. *(The current implementation keeps counters in memory per server instance.)*

### 3.12 Versioning & changes

- The version is in the path (`/api/v1`). Additive, backwards-compatible changes (new optional fields, new endpoints, new enum values for *response-only* enums) can ship in v1. Breaking changes require a new version or a deprecation period.
- Deprecated endpoints are marked `DEPRECATED` with a replacement and removal date, and stay functional until removal.
- Every contract change is recorded in [§5 Changelog](#5-changelog) and follows the update rules in `CLAUDE.md` (registry → types → client → mocks → docs → tests).
- Frontend code should tolerate unknown extra fields in responses.

### 3.13 Plain `fetch` (without the shared client)

The shared client is recommended, but any HTTP client works:

```ts
const res = await fetch("/api/v1/expeditions?region=ANTARCTICA&page=1&limit=12", {
  credentials: "include", // send the session cookie
  headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!body.success) {
  // body.error: { code, message, details?, requestId }
  throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message}`);
}
const { data, pagination } = body;
```

---

## 4. Endpoint reference

Endpoints are grouped by area: system & auth, homepage, expeditions, publications, datasets, media, news & activities, search, files, submission & approval, AI, outreach, categories & keywords, admin. Each entry lists: basic information, request (path/query/body with types, validation and defaults), response with a JSON example, the errors it actually returns, and frontend integration notes with a TypeScript example.

## 4.1 System

Health and diagnostics.

<a id="op-gethealth"></a>

### Health check

`GET /api/v1/health`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Health check |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/health` |
| **Operation ID** | `getHealth` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Monitoring / deployment checks |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `getHealth()` — `@/lib/api/system` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Liveness/readiness probe. Verifies the database connection.

#### Request

No parameters or body.

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/health
```

#### Response `200`

Service and database are healthy.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "status": "ok",
    "version": "mock",
    "timestamp": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "checks": {
      "database": "ok"
    }
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  | Returned only when healthy. An unhealthy service answers 503 SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE. |
| `data.status` | `"ok"` | yes |  |  |
| `data.version` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.timestamp` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.checks` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.checks.database` | `"ok"` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |
| 503 | `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | Database unreachable. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Monitoring / deployment checks
- **When to call:** Deployment health checks and uptime monitoring. Not used by pages.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { getHealth } from "@/lib/api/system";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getHealth();
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: INTERNAL_ERROR | SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/health`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.2 Auth

Registration, login/logout (httpOnly session cookie), passwords.

<a id="op-register"></a>

### Register a member account

`POST /api/v1/auth/register`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Register a member account |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/auth/register` |
| **Operation ID** | `register` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 5 requests per 600s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Register (`/register`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `register()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a MEMBER account and logs the user in (sets the session cookie). Staff accounts (RESEARCHER/EDITOR/ADMIN) are created by admins via `POST /admin/users`.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `email` | string (email) | yes |  | max length 254 | Email address (case-insensitive). |
| `password` | string | yes |  | length 10–128 | 10–128 characters. |
| `affiliation` | string | no |  | max length 200 |  |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/register
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Asha Rao",
  "email": "asha@example.org",
  "password": "correct-horse-battery",
  "affiliation": "Demo University"
}
```

#### Response `201`

Account created and logged in.

| Header | Value |
|---|---|
| `Set-Cookie` | `oceanx_session=<opaque>; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=<ttl>` (+ `Secure` in production) |

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": "usr_mock_1001",
      "email": "asha@example.org",
      "name": "Asha Rao",
      "role": "MEMBER",
      "status": "ACTIVE",
      "affiliation": "Demo University",
      "bio": null,
      "avatarUrl": null,
      "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
      "lastLoginAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
    },
    "expiresAt": "2026-01-29T12:00:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.user` | object | yes |  | Full user record. Only returned to the user themself and to admins. |
| `data.user.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.user.email` | string (email) | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.role` | enum | yes | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `data.user.status` | enum | yes | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `data.user.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  | Institution or organisation. |
| `data.user.bio` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.user.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.user.lastLoginAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.expiresAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | When the session cookie expires. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Request came from a disallowed Origin. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | An account with this email already exists (`details.reason = EMAIL_TAKEN`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Register (`/register`)
- **When to call:** On register form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh any cached session state (e.g. call `getSession()` or `router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** None.
- Show `error.details.fieldErrors` next to inputs on 422.
- On 409 show “An account with this email already exists” with a link to /login.
- Disable the submit button while the request is pending.

```ts
import { register } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await register({
    name: "Asha Rao",
    email: "asha@example.org",
    password: "correct-horse-battery",
    affiliation: "Demo University"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/auth/register`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"name":"Asha Rao","email":"asha@example.org","password":"correct-horse-battery","affiliation":"Demo University"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

### Log in

`POST /api/v1/auth/login`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Log in |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/auth/login` |
| **Operation ID** | `login` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 10 requests per 300s per IP address |

| **Client function** | `login()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Verifies credentials and sets the httpOnly session cookie. The response never contains a token.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `email` | string (email) | yes |  | max length 254 | Email address (case-insensitive). |
| `password` | string | yes |  | length 1–128 |  |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/login
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "member@demo.oceanx.local",
  "password": "demo-password-123"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Logged in.

| Header | Value |
|---|---|
| `Set-Cookie` | `oceanx_session=<opaque>; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=<ttl>` (+ `Secure` in production) |

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "user": {
      "id": "usr_demo_member",
      "email": "member@demo.oceanx.local",
      "name": "Demo Member",
      "role": "MEMBER",
      "status": "ACTIVE",
      "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
      "bio": "Demo Member is a demo account. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.",
      "avatarUrl": null,
      "createdAt": "2025-01-10T09:00:00.000Z",
      "lastLoginAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
    },
    "expiresAt": "2026-01-29T12:00:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.user` | object | yes |  | Full user record. Only returned to the user themself and to admins. |
| `data.user.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.user.email` | string (email) | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.role` | enum | yes | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `data.user.status` | enum | yes | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `data.user.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  | Institution or organisation. |
| `data.user.bio` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.user.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.user.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.user.lastLoginAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.expiresAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | When the session cookie expires. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Invalid email or password. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Account is suspended (`details.reason = ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED`), or disallowed Origin. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Login (`/login`)
- **When to call:** On login form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, navigate and refresh session-dependent UI (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** None.
- Show a generic “Invalid email or password” on 401 — do not reveal which field was wrong.
- The browser stores the cookie automatically; do NOT store anything in localStorage.

```ts
import { login } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await login({
    email: "member@demo.oceanx.local",
    password: "demo-password-123"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

### Log out

`POST /api/v1/auth/logout`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Log out |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/auth/logout` |
| **Operation ID** | `logout` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Global header (search box, user menu)<br>My Account (`/account`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `logout()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Revokes the current session and clears the cookie. Idempotent: succeeds even when not logged in.

#### Request

No parameters or body.

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/logout
```

#### Response `200`

Logged out.

| Header | Value |
|---|---|
| `Set-Cookie` | `oceanx_session=; Max-Age=0` (clears the cookie) |

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "loggedOut": true
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

|---|---|---|---|---|

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Request came from a disallowed Origin. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Global header (search box, user menu), My Account (`/account`)
- **When to call:** When the user clicks “Log out”.
- **Caching:** Never cache. Afterwards call `router.refresh()` and redirect to `/`.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.

```ts
  }
}
```

- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Render a neutral header skeleton until it resolves to avoid a logged-out flash.

| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `changePassword()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Changes the current user's password and revokes all *other* sessions.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `currentPassword` | string | yes |  | length 1–128 |  |
| `newPassword` | string | yes |  | length 10–128 | 10–128 characters. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/password/change
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "currentPassword": "demo-password-123",
  "newPassword": "a-new-long-password"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Password changed.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "changed": true
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.changed` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or `currentPassword` is wrong (`details.reason = INVALID_CURRENT_PASSWORD`). |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Request came from a disallowed Origin. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** My Account (`/account`)
- **When to call:** On change-password form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { changePassword } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await changePassword({
    currentPassword: "demo-password-123",
    newPassword: "a-new-long-password"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/auth/password/change`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"currentPassword":"demo-password-123","newPassword":"a-new-long-password"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-forgotpassword"></a>

### Request a password reset email

`POST /api/v1/auth/password/forgot`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Request a password reset email |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/auth/password/forgot` |
| **Operation ID** | `forgotPassword` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 3 requests per 900s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Forgot / Reset Password (`/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `forgotPassword()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Sends a reset link if the account exists. Always returns 202 so the response does not reveal whether an email is registered. Requires an email provider (not yet configured).

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `email` | string (email) | yes |  | max length 254 | Email address (case-insensitive). |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/password/forgot
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "email": "member@demo.oceanx.local"
}
```

#### Response `202`

Accepted.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "accepted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  | Request accepted. Always returned, to avoid revealing whether an account exists. |
| `data.accepted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Forgot / Reset Password (`/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`)
- **When to call:** On forgot-password form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. Always show “If an account exists, we sent an email”.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { forgotPassword } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await forgotPassword({
    email: "member@demo.oceanx.local"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/auth/password/forgot`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"email":"member@demo.oceanx.local"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

<a id="op-resetpassword"></a>

### Reset password with token

`POST /api/v1/auth/password/reset`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Reset password with token |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/auth/password/reset` |
| **Operation ID** | `resetPassword` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 5 requests per 900s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Forgot / Reset Password (`/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `resetPassword()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Sets a new password using the token from the reset email, and revokes all sessions.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `token` | string | yes |  | length 16–256 | Token from the reset email link. |
| `newPassword` | string | yes |  | length 10–128 | 10–128 characters. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/auth/password/reset
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "token": "demo-reset-token-0123456789",
  "newPassword": "a-new-long-password"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Password reset.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "reset": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.reset` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON, or the token is invalid/expired (`details.reason = INVALID_TOKEN`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Forgot / Reset Password (`/forgot-password`, `/reset-password`)
- **When to call:** On reset-password form submit (token read from the URL).
- **Caching:** Never cache. Redirect to /login on success.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { resetPassword } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await resetPassword({
    token: "demo-reset-token-0123456789",
    newPassword: "a-new-long-password"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie

  body: JSON.stringify({"token":"demo-reset-token-0123456789","newPassword":"a-new-long-password"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
```

## 4.3 Users

The logged-in user's own profile.

<a id="op-updatemyprofile"></a>

### Update my profile

`PATCH /api/v1/users/me`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update my profile |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/users/me` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateMyProfile` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | Any logged-in user |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | My Account (`/account`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `updateMyProfile()` — `@/lib/api/auth` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Updates the logged-in user's own profile fields. Role and email cannot be changed here.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | no |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `affiliation` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `bio` | string \| null | no |  | max length 2000 |  |
| `avatarUrl` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/users/me
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Studies",
  "bio": "Interested in sea ice."
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated user.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "usr_demo_member",
    "email": "member@demo.oceanx.local",
    "name": "Demo Member",
    "role": "MEMBER",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Studies",
    "bio": "Interested in sea ice.",
    "avatarUrl": null,
    "createdAt": "2025-01-10T09:00:00.000Z",
    "lastLoginAt": null
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  | Full user record. Only returned to the user themself and to admins. |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.email` | string (email) | yes |  |  |
| `data.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.role` | enum | yes | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `data.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  | Institution or organisation. |
| `data.bio` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.lastLoginAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Request came from a disallowed Origin. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** My Account (`/account`)
- **When to call:** On profile form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. Update local session state with the returned user.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { updateMyProfile } from "@/lib/api/auth";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await updateMyProfile({
    affiliation: "Demo Institute of Polar Studies",
    bio: "Interested in sea ice."
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/users/me`, {
  method: "PATCH",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"affiliation":"Demo Institute of Polar Studies","bio":"Interested in sea ice."}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Home (`/`)
- **When to call:** Once, in the homepage Server Component.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; up to ~5 min staleness is fine. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** None.
- Each array may be empty — hide that section instead of showing an empty grid.

```ts
import { getHomePageData } from "@/lib/api/home";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getHomePageData();
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/home`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.5 Expeditions

Polar expeditions.

<a id="op-listexpeditions"></a>

### List expeditions

`GET /api/v1/expeditions`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List expeditions |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/expeditions` |
| **Operation ID** | `listExpeditions` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Expedition Explorer (`/expeditions`)<br>Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getExpeditions()` — `@/lib/api/expeditions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated list of PUBLISHED expeditions with filters for region, phase, year, category and keyword.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `12` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Case-insensitive text search over title and summary fields. |
| `phase` | enum | no |  | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Field status of the expedition (not the editorial status). |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Category slug. |
| `keyword` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Keyword slug. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Staff only (EDITOR/ADMIN): list records in another workflow status. Anyone else gets 403 if this is set to anything other than PUBLISHED. |
| `year` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Exact year. Overrides yearFrom/yearTo. |
| `yearFrom` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Inclusive lower bound. |
| `yearTo` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Inclusive upper bound. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `title`, `startDate` | `newest`/`oldest` sort by year then start date. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/expeditions?region=ANTARCTICA&page=1&limit=12
```

#### Response `200`

Expeditions.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].summary` | string | yes |  | 1–3 sentence plain-text summary for cards. |
| `data[].region` | enum | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].phase` | enum | yes | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Field status of the expedition (not the editorial status). |
| `data[].year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data[].startDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data[].endDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data[].station` | string \| null | yes |  | Research station / base name. |
| `data[].vessel` | string \| null | yes |  | Research vessel, if any. |
| `data[].coverImage` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].coverImage.alt` | string | yes | max length 300 | Alt text. Required for accessibility. |
| `data[].coverImage.width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.credit` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Photographer / source credit line. |
| `data[].categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | `status` filter used without an EDITOR/ADMIN session. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Expedition Explorer (`/expeditions`), Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On page load and whenever filters, sort or page change.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; ~1 min staleness is fine. See §3.8. Do not cache responses fetched with a staff session (`status` filter).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Pagination: send `page`/`limit`; render pages from `pagination.totalPages`, and use `hasNextPage` for “Load more”.
- Filtering: mirror filters in the URL query string (e.g. `/expeditions?region=ARCTIC&page=2`) so results are shareable; reset `page` to 1 when a filter changes.
- Loading: show skeleton cards matching `limit`; on error show a retry button; when `data` is empty show an empty state, not an error.
- Map view: request `limit=100` and plot `location` from the detail endpoint, or group cards by `region`.

```ts
import { getExpeditions } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getExpeditions({
    region: "ANTARCTICA",
    page: 1,
    limit: 12
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/expeditions?region=ANTARCTICA&page=1&limit=12`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

### Get an expedition

`GET /api/v1/expeditions/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Get an expedition |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/expeditions/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `getExpedition` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |

| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |

| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getExpedition()` — `@/lib/api/expeditions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

```json
    "publishedAt": "2024-02-15T09:00:00.000Z",
    "createdAt": "2024-01-02T09:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2025-06-01T08:00:00.000Z",
    "description": "## About this demo record\n\nDemonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.\n\nIn a real record, this Markdown section would describe the expedition's context, team, route and outcomes.\n\n## Placeholder section\n\n- Item one\n- Item two",
    "objectives": [
      "Placeholder objective one",
      "Placeholder objective two",
      "Placeholder objective three"
    ],
    "leadInstitution": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
    "chiefScientist": "Demo Scientist",
    "participantCount": 15,
    "location": {
      "latitude": -69.5,
      "longitude": 76.2,
      "label": "Demo coastal site A"
    },
    "counts": {
      "publications": 2,
      "datasets": 1,
      "media": 3
    }
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.summary` | string | yes |  | 1–3 sentence plain-text summary for cards. |
| `data.region` | enum | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data.phase` | enum | yes | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Field status of the expedition (not the editorial status). |
| `data.year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data.startDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data.endDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data.station` | string \| null | yes |  | Research station / base name. |
| `data.vessel` | string \| null | yes |  | Research vessel, if any. |
| `data.coverImage` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.coverImage.alt` | string | yes | max length 300 | Alt text. Required for accessibility. |
| `data.coverImage.width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.credit` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Photographer / source credit line. |
| `data.categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.description` | string | yes |  | Long description in Markdown. |
| `data.objectives` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.leadInstitution` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.chiefScientist` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.participantCount` | integer \| null | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.location` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.location.latitude` | number | yes | -90–90 |  |
| `data.location.longitude` | number | yes | -180–180 |  |
| `data.location.label` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Human-readable place name. |
| `data.counts` | object | yes |  | Published related records. Fetch them with `?expeditionId=` on the publications/datasets/media list endpoints. |
| `data.counts.publications` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.datasets` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.media` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | No published expedition with this ID/slug (or not visible to you). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On detail page load (Server Component), using the slug from the URL.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; 1–5 min staleness is fine. Drafts fetched with a staff/owner session must not be cached or shared. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Accepts the slug from the page URL directly — no need to look up the ID first.
- On 404 render the Next.js `notFound()` page.
- Body fields are Markdown: render with a Markdown renderer that sanitises HTML.
- Related content: call getPublications/getDatasets/getMediaAssets with `{ expeditionId: expedition.id }` in parallel; hide a section when its count is 0.

```ts
import { getExpedition } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getExpedition("demo-antarctic-coastal-ice-survey");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/expeditions/demo-antarctic-coastal-ice-survey`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-createexpedition"></a>

### Create an expedition

`POST /api/v1/expeditions`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create an expedition |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/expeditions` |
| **Operation ID** | `createExpedition` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createExpedition()` — `@/lib/api/expeditions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a record in `DRAFT` status owned by the caller. Researchers then request publication with `POST /submissions`; EDITOR/ADMIN may publish directly with `PATCH … { status: "PUBLISHED" }`.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | yes |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | yes |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `description` | string | yes |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `region` | enum | yes |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `phase` | enum | no |  | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Defaults to PLANNED on create. |
| `year` | integer | yes |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `startDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `endDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `station` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `vessel` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `leadInstitution` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `chiefScientist` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `participantCount` | integer \| null | no |  | 0–10000 |  |
| `objectives` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `location` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `location.latitude` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `location.longitude` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `location.label` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `coverImage` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.url` | string | yes |  | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `coverImage.alt` | string | yes |  | length 1–300 |  |
| `coverImage.width` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.height` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/expeditions
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Demo Expedition: Sea Ice Thickness Pilot",
  "summary": "Demonstration record created through the API.",
  "description": "Placeholder description used to demonstrate the create endpoint.",
  "region": "ARCTIC",
  "year": 2026,
  "categoryIds": [
    "cat_sea_ice"
  ],
  "keywords": [
    "sea ice"
  ]
}
```

#### Response `201`

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug already in use (`details.reason = SLUG_TAKEN`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On create form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createExpedition } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createExpedition({
    title: "Demo Expedition: Sea Ice Thickness Pilot",
    summary: "Demonstration record created through the API.",
    description: "Placeholder description used to demonstrate the create endpoint.",
    region: "ARCTIC",
    year: 2026,
    categoryIds: [
      "cat_sea_ice"
    ],
    keywords: [
      "sea ice"
    ]
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/expeditions`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"title":"Demo Expedition: Sea Ice Thickness Pilot","summary":"Demonstration record created through the API.","description":"Placeholder description used to demonstrate the create endpoint.","region":"ARCTIC","year":2026,"categoryIds":["cat_sea_ice"],"keywords":["sea ice"]}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-updateexpedition"></a>

### Update an expedition

`PATCH /api/v1/expeditions/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update an expedition |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/expeditions/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateExpedition` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updateExpedition()` — `@/lib/api/expeditions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Partial update. The owner may edit while the record is `DRAFT` or `CHANGES_REQUESTED`; EDITOR/ADMIN may edit any time and may set `status` directly. Keyword labels and category IDs replace the existing set when provided.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | no |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `description` | string | no |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `phase` | enum | no |  | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Defaults to PLANNED on create. |
| `year` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `startDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `endDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `station` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `vessel` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `leadInstitution` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `chiefScientist` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `participantCount` | integer \| null | no |  | 0–10000 |  |
| `objectives` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `location` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `location.latitude` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `location.longitude` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `location.label` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `coverImage` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.url` | string | yes |  | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `coverImage.alt` | string | yes |  | length 1–300 |  |
| `coverImage.width` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.height` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Set the workflow status directly (e.g. ARCHIVED). Researchers must use POST /submissions to request publication. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/expeditions/exp_demo_01
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "phase": "COMPLETED"
}
```

```json
      "media": 3
    }
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.summary` | string | yes |  | 1–3 sentence plain-text summary for cards. |
| `data.region` | enum | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data.phase` | enum | yes | One of: `PLANNED`, `ONGOING`, `COMPLETED` | Field status of the expedition (not the editorial status). |
| `data.year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data.startDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data.endDate` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data.station` | string \| null | yes |  | Research station / base name. |
| `data.vessel` | string \| null | yes |  | Research vessel, if any. |
| `data.coverImage` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.coverImage.alt` | string | yes | max length 300 | Alt text. Required for accessibility. |
| `data.coverImage.width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.coverImage.credit` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Photographer / source credit line. |
| `data.categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.description` | string | yes |  | Long description in Markdown. |
| `data.objectives` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.leadInstitution` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.chiefScientist` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.participantCount` | integer \| null | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.location` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.location.latitude` | number | yes | -90–90 |  |
| `data.location.longitude` | number | yes | -180–180 |  |
| `data.location.label` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Human-readable place name. |
| `data.counts` | object | yes |  | Published related records. Fetch them with `?expeditionId=` on the publications/datasets/media list endpoints. |
| `data.counts.publications` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.datasets` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.media` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Expedition not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug already in use. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On edit form save.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { updateExpedition } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await updateExpedition("exp_demo_01", {
    phase: "COMPLETED"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/expeditions/exp_demo_01`, {
  method: "PATCH",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"phase":"COMPLETED"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-deleteexpedition"></a>

### Archive an expedition

`DELETE /api/v1/expeditions/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Archive an expedition |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/expeditions/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deleteExpedition` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `deleteExpedition()` — `@/lib/api/expeditions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Archives (soft-deletes) the record: it disappears from public endpoints and search. EDITOR/ADMIN, or the owner of a `DRAFT`.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/expeditions/exp_demo_09
```

#### Response `200`

Archived.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "exp_demo_09",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Expedition not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** After the user confirms deletion.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deleteExpedition } from "@/lib/api/expeditions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deleteExpedition("exp_demo_09");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```
