## 4.6 Publications

Research publications repository.

<a id="op-listpublications"></a>

### List research publications

`GET /api/v1/publications`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List research publications |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/publications` |
| **Operation ID** | `listPublications` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Research Publications (`/research`)<br>Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`)<br>Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getPublications()` — `@/lib/api/publications` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated list of PUBLISHED publications. Filter by type, year range, region, author, category, keyword or expedition.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `12` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Searches title, abstract and author names. |
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `JOURNAL_ARTICLE`, `CONFERENCE_PAPER`, `REPORT`, `THESIS`, `BOOK_CHAPTER`, `TECHNICAL_NOTE`, `DATA_PAPER`, `OTHER` |  |
| `author` | string | no |  | length 2–100 | Author name contains (case-insensitive). |
| `expeditionId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Only records linked to this expedition (ID). |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Category slug. |
| `keyword` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Keyword slug. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Staff only (EDITOR/ADMIN): list records in another workflow status. Anyone else gets 403 if this is set to anything other than PUBLISHED. |
| `year` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Exact year. Overrides yearFrom/yearTo. |
| `yearFrom` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Inclusive lower bound. |
| `yearTo` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 | Inclusive upper bound. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `title` | `newest` = by year, descending. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/publications?type=JOURNAL_ARTICLE&yearFrom=2020&page=1&limit=10
```

#### Response `200`

Publications.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].abstractPreview` | string | yes |  | First ~300 characters of the abstract, plain text. |
| `data[].authors` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].authors[].name` | string | yes | length 1–200 |  |
| `data[].authors[].affiliation` | string \| null | yes | max length 300 |  |
| `data[].authors[].orcid` | string \| null | yes | pattern `^\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{3}[\dX]$` |  |
| `data[].type` | enum | yes | One of: `JOURNAL_ARTICLE`, `CONFERENCE_PAPER`, `REPORT`, `THESIS`, `BOOK_CHAPTER`, `TECHNICAL_NOTE`, `DATA_PAPER`, `OTHER` |  |
| `data[].year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data[].venue` | string \| null | yes |  | Journal, conference or publisher series. |
| `data[].doi` | string \| null | yes |  | DOI without the `https://doi.org/` prefix. |
| `data[].region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].accessLevel` | enum | yes | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `data[].hasFullText` | boolean | yes |  | True when a PDF is attached. |
| `data[].categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | `status` filter used without an EDITOR/ADMIN session. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Research Publications (`/research`), Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`), Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On page load and on filter/page change. On expedition pages, with `expeditionId`.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; ~1 min staleness is fine. See §3.8. Do not cache responses fetched with a staff session (`status` filter).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Pagination: send `page`/`limit`; render pages from `pagination.totalPages`, and use `hasNextPage` for “Load more”.
- Filtering: mirror filters in the URL query string (e.g. `/expeditions?region=ARCTIC&page=2`) so results are shareable; reset `page` to 1 when a filter changes.
- Loading: show skeleton cards matching `limit`; on error show a retry button; when `data` is empty show an empty state, not an error.
- Debounce the `q`/`author` text inputs (~300 ms) before refetching.

```ts
import { getPublications } from "@/lib/api/publications";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getPublications({
    type: "JOURNAL_ARTICLE",
    yearFrom: 2020,
    page: 1,
    limit: 10
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/publications?type=JOURNAL_ARTICLE&yearFrom=2020&page=1&limit=10`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

```json
      "fileName": "demo-paper-1-sea-ice-variability.pdf",
      "contentType": "application/pdf",
      "sizeBytes": 862345,
      "accessLevel": "OPEN",
      "downloadUrl": "/api/v1/files/file_demo_41/download"
    },

      "slug": "demo-antarctic-coastal-ice-survey",
      "title": "Demo Expedition: Antarctic Coastal Ice Survey",
      "year": 2024
    },

      {
        "id": "ds_demo_01",
        "slug": "demo-coastal-ice-elevation-points",
        "title": "Demo Dataset: Coastal Ice Elevation Points"
      }
    ],
    "citation": {
      "apa": "A, D. A., B, D. A., & Z, D. A. (2019). Demo Paper 1: Placeholder Study on Sea ice variability. Demo Journal of Polar Studies, 10(1), 100–112. https://doi.org/10.5555/oceanx-demo.1",
      "bibtex": "@article{a2019demopaper,\n  title = {Demo Paper 1: Placeholder Study on Sea ice variability},\n  author = {Demo Author A and Demo Author B and Demo Author Z},\n  year = {2019},\n  journal = {Demo Journal of Polar Studies},\n  volume = {10},\n  number = {1},\n  pages = {100–112},\n  doi = {10.5555/oceanx-demo.1}\n}"
    },
    "aiSummary": {
      "id": "ai_demo_03",
      "text": "**Demo AI-assisted text.** Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.\n\nThis placeholder stands in for a research summary written for general public.",
      "audience": "GENERAL_PUBLIC",
      "language": "en",
      "model": "claude-opus-5-5",
      "reviewedAt": "2025-06-01T08:00:00.000Z",
      "disclaimer": "This text was drafted with AI assistance from the linked source records and reviewed by portal staff. Refer to the original sources for authoritative scientific information."
    }
  }
}
```

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | No published publication with this ID/slug (or not visible to you). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Publication Detail (`/research/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On detail page load.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; 1–5 min staleness is fine. Drafts fetched with a staff/owner session must not be cached or shared. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Accepts the slug from the page URL directly — no need to look up the ID first.
- On 404 render the Next.js `notFound()` page.
- Body fields are Markdown: render with a Markdown renderer that sanitises HTML.
- Show `citation.apa` with a copy button; offer `citation.bibtex` as a download.
- If `aiSummary` is present, label it as AI-assisted and always show `aiSummary.disclaimer`.
- PDF link: use `pdf.downloadUrl` as a plain `<a href>`; for REGISTERED access prompt login on 401.

```ts
import { getPublication } from "@/lib/api/publications";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getPublication("pub_demo_01");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/publications/pub_demo_01`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-createpublication"></a>

### Create a publication

`POST /api/v1/publications`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a publication |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/publications` |
| **Operation ID** | `createPublication` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createPublication()` — `@/lib/api/publications` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a record in `DRAFT` status owned by the caller. Researchers then request publication with `POST /submissions`; EDITOR/ADMIN may publish directly with `PATCH … { status: "PUBLISHED" }`. Attach a PDF by uploading it first (`POST /uploads`, purpose `PUBLICATION_PDF`) and passing `pdfFileId`.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | yes |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `abstract` | string | yes |  | length 20–10000 |  |
| `authors` | object[] | yes |  | min 1 item(s); max 100 items |  |
| `authors[].name` | string | yes |  | length 1–200 |  |
| `authors[].affiliation` | string \| null | no |  | max length 300 |  |
| `authors[].orcid` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{3}[\dX]$` |  |
| `type` | enum | yes |  | One of: `JOURNAL_ARTICLE`, `CONFERENCE_PAPER`, `REPORT`, `THESIS`, `BOOK_CHAPTER`, `TECHNICAL_NOTE`, `DATA_PAPER`, `OTHER` |  |
| `year` | integer | yes |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `publicationDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `venue` | string \| null | no |  | max length 300 |  |
| `volume` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `issue` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `pages` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `publisher` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `doi` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^10\.\d{4,9}\/\S+$` |  |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `pdfFileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | ID of an uploaded file (purpose PUBLICATION_PDF). |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | Defaults to OPEN. |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `datasetIds` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/publications
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Demo Paper: Placeholder Study Title",
  "abstract": "Placeholder abstract used to demonstrate the create endpoint. Not a real study.",
  "authors": [
    {
      "name": "Demo Author A"
    }
  ],
  "type": "JOURNAL_ARTICLE",
  "year": 2026
}
```

#### Response `201`

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug or DOI already exists (`details.reason = SLUG_TAKEN \| DOI_TAKEN`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On create form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createPublication } from "@/lib/api/publications";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createPublication({
    title: "Demo Paper: Placeholder Study Title",
    abstract: "Placeholder abstract used to demonstrate the create endpoint. Not a real study.",
    authors: [
      {
        name: "Demo Author A"
      }
    ],
    type: "JOURNAL_ARTICLE",
    year: 2026
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/publications`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"title":"Demo Paper: Placeholder Study Title","abstract":"Placeholder abstract used to demonstrate the create endpoint. Not a real study.","authors":[{"name":"Demo Author A"}],"type":"JOURNAL_ARTICLE","year":2026}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-updatepublication"></a>

### Update a publication

`PATCH /api/v1/publications/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update a publication |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/publications/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updatePublication` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updatePublication()` — `@/lib/api/publications` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Partial update. The owner may edit while the record is `DRAFT` or `CHANGES_REQUESTED`; EDITOR/ADMIN may edit any time and may set `status` directly. Keyword labels and category IDs replace the existing set when provided.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `abstract` | string | no |  | length 20–10000 |  |
| `authors` | object[] | no |  | min 1 item(s); max 100 items |  |
| `authors[].name` | string | yes |  | length 1–200 |  |
| `authors[].affiliation` | string \| null | no |  | max length 300 |  |
| `authors[].orcid` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{3}[\dX]$` |  |
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `JOURNAL_ARTICLE`, `CONFERENCE_PAPER`, `REPORT`, `THESIS`, `BOOK_CHAPTER`, `TECHNICAL_NOTE`, `DATA_PAPER`, `OTHER` |  |
| `year` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `publicationDate` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `venue` | string \| null | no |  | max length 300 |  |
| `volume` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `issue` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `pages` | string \| null | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `publisher` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `doi` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^10\.\d{4,9}\/\S+$` |  |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `pdfFileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | ID of an uploaded file (purpose PUBLICATION_PDF). |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | Defaults to OPEN. |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `datasetIds` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Set the workflow status directly (e.g. ARCHIVED). Researchers must use POST /submissions to request publication. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/publications/pub_demo_01
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "venue": "Demo Journal of Polar Studies"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated.

| `data.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.abstractPreview` | string | yes |  | First ~300 characters of the abstract, plain text. |

| `data.authors[].affiliation` | string \| null | yes | max length 300 |  |

| `data.type` | enum | yes | One of: `JOURNAL_ARTICLE`, `CONFERENCE_PAPER`, `REPORT`, `THESIS`, `BOOK_CHAPTER`, `TECHNICAL_NOTE`, `DATA_PAPER`, `OTHER` |  |
| `data.year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data.venue` | string \| null | yes |  | Journal, conference or publisher series. |

| `data.region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data.accessLevel` | enum | yes | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `data.hasFullText` | boolean | yes |  | True when a PDF is attached. |

| `data.categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Publication not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug or DOI already exists. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On edit form save.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { updatePublication } from "@/lib/api/publications";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await updatePublication("pub_demo_01", {
    venue: "Demo Journal of Polar Studies"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/publications/pub_demo_01`, {
  method: "PATCH",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"venue":"Demo Journal of Polar Studies"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-deletepublication"></a>

### Archive a publication

`DELETE /api/v1/publications/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Archive a publication |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/publications/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deletePublication` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `deletePublication()` — `@/lib/api/publications` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Archives (soft-deletes) the record: it disappears from public endpoints and search. EDITOR/ADMIN, or the owner of a `DRAFT`.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/publications/pub_demo_14
```

#### Response `200`

Archived.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "pub_demo_14",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Publication not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** After the user confirms deletion.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deletePublication } from "@/lib/api/publications";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deletePublication("pub_demo_14");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/publications/pub_demo_14`, {
  method: "DELETE",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.7 Datasets

Scientific datasets repository.

<a id="op-listdatasets"></a>

### List scientific datasets

`GET /api/v1/datasets`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List scientific datasets |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/datasets` |
| **Operation ID** | `listDatasets` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Data Repository (`/datasets`)<br>Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`)<br>Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getDatasets()` — `@/lib/api/datasets` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated list of PUBLISHED datasets. Filter by region, access level, format, parameter, temporal coverage, category, keyword or expedition.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `12` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Searches title, summary and parameters. |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `format` | string | no |  | length 1–40 | File format, case-insensitive (e.g. `netcdf`). |
| `parameter` | string | no |  | length 2–100 | Parameter name contains. |
| `expeditionId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Only records linked to this expedition (ID). |
| `from` | string (date) | no |  |  | Temporal coverage overlaps on/after this date. |
| `to` | string (date) | no |  |  | Temporal coverage overlaps on/before this date. |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Category slug. |
| `keyword` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Keyword slug. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Staff only (EDITOR/ADMIN): list records in another workflow status. Anyone else gets 403 if this is set to anything other than PUBLISHED. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `title`, `downloads` |  |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/datasets?region=ARCTIC&accessLevel=OPEN&page=1&limit=12
```

#### Response `200`

Datasets.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].summary` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].region` | enum | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].parameters` | string[] | yes |  | Measured variables, e.g. `Sea surface temperature`. |
| `data[].formats` | string[] | yes |  | File formats, e.g. `NetCDF`, `CSV`. |
| `data[].temporalCoverage` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data[].temporalCoverage.start` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data[].temporalCoverage.end` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data[].accessLevel` | enum | yes | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `data[].license` | string | yes |  | SPDX identifier or license name, e.g. `CC-BY-4.0`. |
| `data[].doi` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].version` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].fileCount` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].totalSizeBytes` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].downloadCount` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | `status` filter used without an EDITOR/ADMIN session. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Data Repository (`/datasets`), Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`), Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On page load and on filter/page change.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; ~1 min staleness is fine. See §3.8. Do not cache responses fetched with a staff session (`status` filter).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Pagination: send `page`/`limit`; render pages from `pagination.totalPages`, and use `hasNextPage` for “Load more”.
- Filtering: mirror filters in the URL query string (e.g. `/expeditions?region=ARCTIC&page=2`) so results are shareable; reset `page` to 1 when a filter changes.
- Loading: show skeleton cards matching `limit`; on error show a retry button; when `data` is empty show an empty state, not an error.
- Show an access badge from `accessLevel` (OPEN / Login required / Restricted).
- Format sizes from `totalSizeBytes` on the client (e.g. 1.2 GB).

```ts
import { getDatasets } from "@/lib/api/datasets";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getDatasets({
    region: "ARCTIC",
    accessLevel: "OPEN",
    page: 1,
    limit: 12
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/datasets?region=ARCTIC&accessLevel=OPEN&page=1&limit=12`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-getdataset"></a>

### Get a dataset

`GET /api/v1/datasets/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Get a dataset |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/datasets/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `getDataset` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Dataset Detail (`/datasets/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getDataset()` — `@/lib/api/datasets` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Full dataset by ID or slug: description, coverage, files with download links, citation, related publications.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–160 | Record ID or slug. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/datasets/ds_demo_01
```

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | No published dataset with this ID/slug (or not visible to you). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Dataset Detail (`/datasets/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On detail page load.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; 1–5 min staleness is fine. Drafts fetched with a staff/owner session must not be cached or shared. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Accepts the slug from the page URL directly — no need to look up the ID first.
- On 404 render the Next.js `notFound()` page.
- Body fields are Markdown: render with a Markdown renderer that sanitises HTML.
- Downloads: render each `files[].downloadUrl` as `<a href download>` — the browser sends the session cookie automatically.
- REGISTERED: if the user is logged out, show “Log in to download” instead of the link. RESTRICTED: show `contact` details instead.
- Map: draw `spatialCoverage` as a rectangle when present.

```ts
import { getDataset } from "@/lib/api/datasets";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getDataset("ds_demo_01");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/datasets/ds_demo_01`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-createdataset"></a>

### Create a dataset

`POST /api/v1/datasets`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a dataset |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/datasets` |
| **Operation ID** | `createDataset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createDataset()` — `@/lib/api/datasets` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a record in `DRAFT` status owned by the caller. Researchers then request publication with `POST /submissions`; EDITOR/ADMIN may publish directly with `PATCH … { status: "PUBLISHED" }`. Upload data files first (`POST /uploads`, purpose `DATASET_FILE`) and pass their `fileIds`.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | yes |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | yes |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `description` | string | yes |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `region` | enum | yes |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `parameters` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `formats` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `temporalCoverage` | object | no |  |  |  |
| `temporalCoverage.start` | string (date) \| null | yes |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `temporalCoverage.end` | string (date) \| null | yes |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `spatialCoverage` | object \| null | no |  |  | Geographic bounding box in decimal degrees (WGS 84). |
| `spatialCoverage.west` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.south` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.east` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.north` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `license` | string | yes |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `doi` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^10\.\d{4,9}\/\S+$` |  |
| `version` | string \| null | no |  | max length 40 |  |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | Defaults to OPEN. |
| `fileIds` | string[] | no |  | max 200 items | IDs of uploaded files (purpose DATASET_FILE). |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `publicationIds` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `contact` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `contact.name` | string | yes |  |  |  |
| `contact.email` | string (email) \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `contact.organization` | string \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/datasets
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Demo Dataset: Placeholder Temperature Series",
  "summary": "Demonstration dataset record created through the API.",
  "description": "Placeholder description. Contains no real measurements.",
  "region": "ANTARCTICA",
  "license": "CC-BY-4.0",
  "formats": [
    "CSV"
  ]
}
```

#### Response `201`

Created (DRAFT).

```json
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "ds_mock_1004",

    "title": "Demo Dataset: Placeholder Temperature Series",
```

| `data.formats` | string[] | yes |  | File formats, e.g. `NetCDF`, `CSV`. |
| `data.temporalCoverage` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.temporalCoverage.start` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |

| `data.version` | string \| null | yes |  |  |

| `data.totalSizeBytes` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.downloadCount` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

| `data.categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.description` | string | yes |  | Markdown. Methods, processing, quality notes. |
| `data.spatialCoverage` | object \| null | yes |  | Geographic bounding box in decimal degrees (WGS 84). |
| `data.spatialCoverage.west` | number | yes | -180–180 |  |
| `data.spatialCoverage.south` | number | yes | -90–90 |  |
| `data.spatialCoverage.east` | number | yes | -180–180 |  |
| `data.spatialCoverage.north` | number | yes | -90–90 |  |

| `data.files[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.files[].fileName` | string | yes |  |  |

| `data.files[].sizeBytes` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.files[].accessLevel` | enum | yes | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `data.files[].downloadUrl` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Use directly as a link `href`. Points to `GET /api/v1/files/{id}/download`, which enforces access rules. |
| `data.expedition` | object \| null | yes |  |  |

| `data.expedition.title` | string | yes |  |  |

| `data.relatedPublications[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.relatedPublications[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.relatedPublications[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.relatedPublications[].year` | integer | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug or DOI already exists. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On create form submit (after files are uploaded).
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createDataset } from "@/lib/api/datasets";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createDataset({
    title: "Demo Dataset: Placeholder Temperature Series",
    summary: "Demonstration dataset record created through the API.",
    description: "Placeholder description. Contains no real measurements.",
    region: "ANTARCTICA",
    license: "CC-BY-4.0",
    formats: [
      "CSV"
    ]
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/datasets`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"title":"Demo Dataset: Placeholder Temperature Series","summary":"Demonstration dataset record created through the API.","description":"Placeholder description. Contains no real measurements.","region":"ANTARCTICA","license":"CC-BY-4.0","formats":["CSV"]}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-updatedataset"></a>

### Update a dataset

`PATCH /api/v1/datasets/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update a dataset |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/datasets/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateDataset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updateDataset()` — `@/lib/api/datasets` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Partial update. The owner may edit while the record is `DRAFT` or `CHANGES_REQUESTED`; EDITOR/ADMIN may edit any time and may set `status` directly. Keyword labels and category IDs replace the existing set when provided. `fileIds` replaces the attached file set when provided.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | no |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `description` | string | no |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `parameters` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `formats` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `temporalCoverage` | object | no |  |  |  |
| `temporalCoverage.start` | string (date) \| null | yes |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `temporalCoverage.end` | string (date) \| null | yes |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `spatialCoverage` | object \| null | no |  |  | Geographic bounding box in decimal degrees (WGS 84). |
| `spatialCoverage.west` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.south` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.east` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `spatialCoverage.north` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `license` | string | no |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `doi` | string \| null | no |  | pattern `^10\.\d{4,9}\/\S+$` |  |
| `version` | string \| null | no |  | max length 40 |  |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | Defaults to OPEN. |
| `fileIds` | string[] | no |  | max 200 items | IDs of uploaded files (purpose DATASET_FILE). |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `publicationIds` | string[] | no |  | max 50 items |  |
| `contact` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `contact.name` | string | yes |  |  |  |
| `contact.email` | string (email) \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `contact.organization` | string \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Set the workflow status directly (e.g. ARCHIVED). Researchers must use POST /submissions to request publication. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/datasets/ds_demo_01
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "version": "1.1"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated.

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Dataset not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug or DOI already exists. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On edit form save.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { updateDataset } from "@/lib/api/datasets";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await updateDataset("ds_demo_01", {
    version: "1.1"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/datasets/ds_demo_01`, {
  method: "PATCH",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"version":"1.1"}),
});
const body = await res.json();

console.log(body.data);
```

<a id="op-deletedataset"></a>

### Archive a dataset

`DELETE /api/v1/datasets/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Archive a dataset |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/datasets/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deleteDataset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `deleteDataset()` — `@/lib/api/datasets` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Archives (soft-deletes) the record: it disappears from public endpoints and search. EDITOR/ADMIN, or the owner of a `DRAFT`.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/datasets/ds_demo_10
```

#### Response `200`

Archived.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "ds_demo_10",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Dataset not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** After the user confirms deletion.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deleteDataset } from "@/lib/api/datasets";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deleteDataset("ds_demo_10");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/datasets/ds_demo_10`, {
  method: "DELETE",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.8 Media

Photo, video, audio and document gallery.

<a id="op-listmediaassets"></a>

### List media assets

`GET /api/v1/media`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List media assets |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/media` |
| **Operation ID** | `listMediaAssets` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Media Gallery (`/gallery`)<br>Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`)<br>Home (`/`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getMediaAssets()` — `@/lib/api/media` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated gallery of PUBLISHED images, videos, audio and documents. Default page size 24.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `24` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Case-insensitive text search over title and summary fields. |
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `IMAGE`, `VIDEO`, `AUDIO`, `DOCUMENT` |  |
| `featured` | boolean | no |  |  | Only featured items (`true`). |
| `expeditionId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Only records linked to this expedition (ID). |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Category slug. |
| `keyword` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Keyword slug. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Staff only (EDITOR/ADMIN): list records in another workflow status. Anyone else gets 403 if this is set to anything other than PUBLISHED. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `capturedAt` |  |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/media?type=IMAGE&region=ANTARCTICA&page=1&limit=24
```

#### Response `200`

Media assets.


> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].type` | enum | yes | One of: `IMAGE`, `VIDEO`, `AUDIO`, `DOCUMENT` |  |
| `data[].url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Full-size file (IMAGE/AUDIO/DOCUMENT) or video file / page URL. |
| `data[].thumbnailUrl` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Always present. Use for grids. |
| `data[].altText` | string | yes |  | Accessible description. Use as `alt`. |
| `data[].width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].durationSeconds` | integer \| null | yes | ≥ 0 | VIDEO/AUDIO only. |
| `data[].region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].credit` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].license` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].capturedAt` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data[].featured` | boolean | yes |  |  |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | `status` filter used without an EDITOR/ADMIN session. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Media Gallery (`/gallery`), Expedition Detail (`/expeditions/[slug]`), Home (`/`)
- **When to call:** On gallery load, filter change, and “Load more” (next page).
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; ~1 min staleness is fine. See §3.8. Do not cache responses fetched with a staff session (`status` filter).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Pagination: send `page`/`limit`; render pages from `pagination.totalPages`, and use `hasNextPage` for “Load more”.
- Filtering: mirror filters in the URL query string (e.g. `/expeditions?region=ARCTIC&page=2`) so results are shareable; reset `page` to 1 when a filter changes.
- Grid: use `thumbnailUrl` + `altText`; open `url` (or `embedUrl` for VIDEO) in a lightbox.
- Use `width`/`height` to reserve space and avoid layout shift.

```ts
import { getMediaAssets } from "@/lib/api/media";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getMediaAssets({
    type: "IMAGE",
    region: "ANTARCTICA",
    page: 1,
    limit: 24
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/media?type=IMAGE&region=ANTARCTICA&page=1&limit=24`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

- **Pages:** Media Detail (`/gallery/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On media detail page load or when a lightbox opens with a shareable URL.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; 1–5 min staleness is fine. Drafts fetched with a staff/owner session must not be cached or shared. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Accepts the slug from the page URL directly — no need to look up the ID first.
- On 404 render the Next.js `notFound()` page.
- Body fields are Markdown: render with a Markdown renderer that sanitises HTML.
- Always display `credit` and `license` with the asset.

```ts
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<a id="op-createmediaasset"></a>

### Create a media asset

`POST /api/v1/media`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a media asset |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/media` |
| **Operation ID** | `createMediaAsset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createMediaAsset()` — `@/lib/api/media` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a record in `DRAFT` status owned by the caller. Researchers then request publication with `POST /submissions`; EDITOR/ADMIN may publish directly with `PATCH … { status: "PUBLISHED" }`. Upload the file first (`POST /uploads`, purpose `MEDIA`) or provide `externalUrl`.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | yes |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `type` | enum | yes |  | One of: `IMAGE`, `VIDEO`, `AUDIO`, `DOCUMENT` |  |
| `fileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Uploaded file (purpose MEDIA). Either fileId or externalUrl is required. |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 | Externally hosted media (e.g. a video page). |
| `embedUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `thumbnailFileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Generated automatically for uploaded images when omitted. |
| `altText` | string | yes |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `description` | string \| null | no |  | max length 10000 |  |
| `credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `license` | string \| null | no |  | max length 100 |  |
| `capturedAt` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `location` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `location.latitude` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `location.longitude` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `location.label` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `featured` | boolean | no |  |  | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Defaults to false. |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/media
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "title": "Demo Photo: Placeholder Ice Edge",
  "type": "IMAGE",
  "externalUrl": "https://example.org/demo/photo.jpg",
  "altText": "Placeholder image of an ice edge",
  "region": "ARCTIC"
}
```

#### Response `201`

Created (DRAFT).

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "med_mock_1005",
    "slug": "demo-photo-placeholder-ice-edge",
    "title": "Demo Photo: Placeholder Ice Edge",
    "type": "IMAGE",
    "url": "https://example.org/demo/photo.jpg",
    "thumbnailUrl": "https://example.org/demo/photo.jpg",
    "altText": "Placeholder image of an ice edge",
    "width": null,
    "height": null,
    "durationSeconds": null,
    "region": "ARCTIC",
    "credit": null,
    "license": null,
    "capturedAt": null,
    "featured": false,
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "publishedAt": null,
    "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "description": null,
    "embedUrl": null,
    "expedition": null,
    "location": null,
    "categories": [],
    "keywords": []
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.type` | enum | yes | One of: `IMAGE`, `VIDEO`, `AUDIO`, `DOCUMENT` |  |
| `data.url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Full-size file (IMAGE/AUDIO/DOCUMENT) or video file / page URL. |
| `data.thumbnailUrl` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Always present. Use for grids. |
| `data.altText` | string | yes |  | Accessible description. Use as `alt`. |
| `data.width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.durationSeconds` | integer \| null | yes | ≥ 0 | VIDEO/AUDIO only. |
| `data.region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data.credit` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.license` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.capturedAt` | string (date) \| null | yes |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `data.featured` | boolean | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.description` | string \| null | yes |  | Markdown caption / story. |
| `data.embedUrl` | string (url) \| null | yes | max length 2048 | Embeddable player URL for externally hosted video (iframe `src`). |
| `data.expedition` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.expedition.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.expedition.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.expedition.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.expedition.year` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data.location` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.location.latitude` | number | yes | -90–90 |  |
| `data.location.longitude` | number | yes | -180–180 |  |
| `data.location.label` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Human-readable place name. |
| `data.categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data.categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.keywords` | string[] | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Referenced file not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug already in use. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On create form submit (after upload).
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createMediaAsset } from "@/lib/api/media";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createMediaAsset({
    title: "Demo Photo: Placeholder Ice Edge",
    type: "IMAGE",
    externalUrl: "https://example.org/demo/photo.jpg",
    altText: "Placeholder image of an ice edge",
    region: "ARCTIC"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/media`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"title":"Demo Photo: Placeholder Ice Edge","type":"IMAGE","externalUrl":"https://example.org/demo/photo.jpg","altText":"Placeholder image of an ice edge","region":"ARCTIC"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-updatemediaasset"></a>

### Update a media asset

`PATCH /api/v1/media/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update a media asset |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/media/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateMediaAsset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updateMediaAsset()` — `@/lib/api/media` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Partial update. The owner may edit while the record is `DRAFT` or `CHANGES_REQUESTED`; EDITOR/ADMIN may edit any time and may set `status` directly. Keyword labels and category IDs replace the existing set when provided. Only EDITOR/ADMIN may set `featured`.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `IMAGE`, `VIDEO`, `AUDIO`, `DOCUMENT` |  |
| `fileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Uploaded file (purpose MEDIA). Either fileId or externalUrl is required. |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 | Externally hosted media (e.g. a video page). |
| `embedUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `thumbnailFileId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Generated automatically for uploaded images when omitted. |
| `altText` | string | no |  | length 3–300 |  |
| `description` | string \| null | no |  | max length 10000 |  |
| `credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `license` | string \| null | no |  | max length 100 |  |
| `capturedAt` | string (date) \| null | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `location` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `location.latitude` | number | yes |  | -90–90 |  |
| `location.longitude` | number | yes |  | -180–180 |  |
| `location.label` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `expeditionId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `featured` | boolean | no |  |  | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Defaults to false. |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Set the workflow status directly (e.g. ARCHIVED). Researchers must use POST /submissions to request publication. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/media/med_demo_01
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "featured": true
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "med_demo_01",
    "slug": "demo-ice-edge-at-dawn",
    "title": "Demo Media: Ice edge at dawn",
    "type": "IMAGE",
    "url": "/demo/polar-1.svg",
    "thumbnailUrl": "/demo/polar-1.svg",
    "altText": "Placeholder graphic standing in for: ice edge at dawn",
    "width": 1600,
    "height": 900,
    "durationSeconds": null,
    "region": "ANTARCTICA",
    "credit": "Demo placeholder",
    "license": "CC-BY-4.0",
    "capturedAt": "2023-01-01",
    "featured": true,
    "status": "PUBLISHED",
    "publishedAt": "2025-01-12T09:00:00.000Z",
    "createdAt": "2025-01-01T09:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "description": "Placeholder caption. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.",
    "embedUrl": null,
    "expedition": {
      "id": "exp_demo_01",
```

```ts
import { updateMediaAsset } from "@/lib/api/media";
```

<a id="op-deletemediaasset"></a>

### Archive a media asset

`DELETE /api/v1/media/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Archive a media asset |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/media/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deleteMediaAsset` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `deleteMediaAsset()` — `@/lib/api/media` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Archives (soft-deletes) the record: it disappears from public endpoints and search. EDITOR/ADMIN, or the owner of a `DRAFT`.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/media/med_demo_20
```

#### Response `200`

Archived.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "med_demo_20",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).
