| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the owner (while DRAFT/CHANGES_REQUESTED) or EDITOR/ADMIN may do this, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Media asset not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** After the user confirms deletion.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deleteMediaAsset } from "@/lib/api/media";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deleteMediaAsset("med_demo_20");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

## 4.9 Activities

Institutional news, events, press releases and outreach programmes.

<a id="op-listactivities"></a>

### List news and activities

`GET /api/v1/activities`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List news and activities |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/activities` |
| **Operation ID** | `listActivities` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | News & Events (`/news`)<br>Home (`/`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getActivities()` — `@/lib/api/activities` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated list of PUBLISHED institutional news, events, press releases, outreach programmes, workshops and announcements. Use `upcoming=true` for an events calendar.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `12` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Case-insensitive text search over title and summary fields. |
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `NEWS`, `EVENT`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `OUTREACH_PROGRAM`, `WORKSHOP`, `ANNOUNCEMENT` | Institutional news and activities. |
| `upcoming` | boolean | no |  |  | `true`: only items with `startsAt` in the future, sorted soonest first. |
| `from` | string (date) | no |  |  | `startsAt` (or `publishedAt` for news) on/after this date. |
| `to` | string (date) | no |  |  | `startsAt` (or `publishedAt` for news) on/before this date. |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Category slug. |
| `keyword` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Keyword slug. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Staff only (EDITOR/ADMIN): list records in another workflow status. Anyone else gets 403 if this is set to anything other than PUBLISHED. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `startsAt` | `newest` = by publishedAt. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/activities?type=EVENT&upcoming=true&page=1&limit=6
```

#### Response `200`

Activities.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "act_demo_09",
      "slug": "online-q-a-with-researchers-demo",
      "type": "EVENT",
      "title": "Online Q&A with Researchers (Demo)",
      "summary": "Placeholder summary for this demo news/activity item. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.",
      "coverImage": {
        "url": "/demo/polar-5.svg",
        "alt": "Placeholder banner for Online Q&A with Researchers (Demo)",
        "width": 1600,
        "height": 900,
        "credit": "Demo placeholder image"
      },
      "startsAt": "2026-01-22T10:00:00.000Z",
      "endsAt": "2026-01-22T11:00:00.000Z",
```

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].type` | enum | yes | One of: `NEWS`, `EVENT`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `OUTREACH_PROGRAM`, `WORKSHOP`, `ANNOUNCEMENT` | Institutional news and activities. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].summary` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage` | object \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.url` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].coverImage.alt` | string | yes | max length 300 | Alt text. Required for accessibility. |
| `data[].coverImage.width` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.height` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].coverImage.credit` | string \| null | yes | max length 200 | Photographer / source credit line. |
| `data[].startsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | Events/workshops/programmes only. |
| `data[].endsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].location` | string \| null | yes |  | Venue name / city, or `Online`. |
| `data[].isOnline` | boolean | yes |  |  |
| `data[].region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].categories` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].categories[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].categories[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | `status` filter used without an EDITOR/ADMIN session. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** News & Events (`/news`), Home (`/`)
- **When to call:** On page load and on tab/filter/page change (e.g. tabs for News | Events | Press).
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; ~1 min staleness is fine. See §3.8. Do not cache responses fetched with a staff session (`status` filter).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Pagination: send `page`/`limit`; render pages from `pagination.totalPages`, and use `hasNextPage` for “Load more”.
- Filtering: mirror filters in the URL query string (e.g. `/expeditions?region=ARCTIC&page=2`) so results are shareable; reset `page` to 1 when a filter changes.
- Loading: show skeleton cards matching `limit`; on error show a retry button; when `data` is empty show an empty state, not an error.
- Dates are UTC ISO strings: format in the user's locale (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`), show `startsAt` for events and `publishedAt` for news.

```ts
import { getActivities } from "@/lib/api/activities";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getActivities({
    type: "EVENT",
    upcoming: true,
    page: 1,
    limit: 6
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/activities?type=EVENT&upcoming=true&page=1&limit=6`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-getactivity"></a>

### Get a news item or activity

`GET /api/v1/activities/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Get a news item or activity |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/activities/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `getActivity` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | News / Event Detail (`/news/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getActivity()` — `@/lib/api/activities` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Full news/event record by ID or slug with Markdown body and attachments.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–160 | Record ID or slug. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/activities/act_demo_01
```

#### Response `200`

Activity.

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | No published activity with this ID/slug. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** News / Event Detail (`/news/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On detail page load.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; 1–5 min staleness is fine. Drafts fetched with a staff/owner session must not be cached or shared. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.
- Accepts the slug from the page URL directly — no need to look up the ID first.
- On 404 render the Next.js `notFound()` page.
- Body fields are Markdown: render with a Markdown renderer that sanitises HTML.
- Show a “Register” button when `registrationUrl` is set and the event has not ended.

```ts
import { getActivity } from "@/lib/api/activities";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getActivity("act_demo_01");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

```ts
});

console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-createactivity"></a>

### Create a news item or activity

`POST /api/v1/activities`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a news item or activity |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/activities` |
| **Operation ID** | `createActivity` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createActivity()` — `@/lib/api/activities` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates a DRAFT news/activity record. Institutional communications are staff-only.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | enum | yes |  | One of: `NEWS`, `EVENT`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `OUTREACH_PROGRAM`, `WORKSHOP`, `ANNOUNCEMENT` | Institutional news and activities. |
| `title` | string | yes |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | yes |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `body` | string | yes |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `coverImage` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.url` | string | yes |  | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `coverImage.alt` | string | yes |  | length 1–300 |  |
| `coverImage.width` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.height` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `startsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | no |  |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `endsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | no |  |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `location` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `isOnline` | boolean | no |  |  |  |
| `registrationUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `attachmentFileIds` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/activities
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "type": "EVENT",
  "title": "Demo Event: Public Lecture Placeholder",
  "summary": "Demonstration event created through the API.",
  "body": "Placeholder event description.",
  "startsAt": "2026-12-10T10:00:00.000Z",
  "endsAt": "2026-12-10T12:00:00.000Z",
  "isOnline": true
}
```

#### Response `201`

Created (DRAFT).

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "act_mock_1006",
    "slug": "demo-event-public-lecture-placeholder",
    "type": "EVENT",
    "title": "Demo Event: Public Lecture Placeholder",
    "summary": "Demonstration event created through the API.",
    "body": "Placeholder event description.",
    "coverImage": null,
    "startsAt": "2026-12-10T10:00:00.000Z",
    "endsAt": "2026-12-10T12:00:00.000Z",
    "location": null,
    "isOnline": true,
    "region": null,
    "categories": [],
    "keywords": [],
    "registrationUrl": null,
    "externalUrl": null,
    "attachments": [],
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "publishedAt": null,
    "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug already in use. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** On create form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache. After success, refresh affected views (`router.refresh()`).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createActivity } from "@/lib/api/activities";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createActivity({
    type: "EVENT",
    title: "Demo Event: Public Lecture Placeholder",
    summary: "Demonstration event created through the API.",
    body: "Placeholder event description.",
    startsAt: "2026-12-10T10:00:00.000Z",
    endsAt: "2026-12-10T12:00:00.000Z",
    isOnline: true
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/activities`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"type":"EVENT","title":"Demo Event: Public Lecture Placeholder","summary":"Demonstration event created through the API.","body":"Placeholder event description.","startsAt":"2026-12-10T10:00:00.000Z","endsAt":"2026-12-10T12:00:00.000Z","isOnline":true}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-updateactivity"></a>

### Update a news item or activity

`PATCH /api/v1/activities/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Update a news item or activity |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/activities/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateActivity` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updateActivity()` — `@/lib/api/activities` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Partial update; EDITOR/ADMIN may also set `status` directly (e.g. PUBLISHED).

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | enum | no |  | One of: `NEWS`, `EVENT`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `OUTREACH_PROGRAM`, `WORKSHOP`, `ANNOUNCEMENT` | Institutional news and activities. |
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from the title when omitted. Must be unique. |
| `summary` | string | no |  | length 10–500 |  |
| `body` | string | no |  | length 10–50000 |  |
| `coverImage` | object \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.url` | string | yes |  | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `coverImage.alt` | string | yes |  | length 1–300 |  |
| `coverImage.width` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.height` | integer \| null | no |  |  |  |
| `coverImage.credit` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `startsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | no |  |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `endsAt` | string (date-time) \| null | no |  |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `location` | string \| null | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `isOnline` | boolean | no |  |  |  |
| `registrationUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `externalUrl` | string (url) \| null | no |  | max length 2048 |  |
| `region` | enum \| null | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `attachmentFileIds` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items |  |
| `categoryIds` | string[] | no |  | max 10 items | Category IDs (max 10). |
| `keywords` | string[] | no |  | max 20 items | Keyword labels (max 20). Unknown labels are created automatically. |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | EDITOR/ADMIN only. Set the workflow status directly (e.g. ARCHIVED). Researchers must use POST /submissions to request publication. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/activities/act_demo_01
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "status": "PUBLISHED"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "act_demo_01",
    "slug": "portal-launch-announcement-demo",
    "type": "NEWS",
    "title": "Portal Launch Announcement (Demo)",
    "summary": "Placeholder summary for this demo news/activity item. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.",
    "coverImage": {

        "slug": "expedition-logistics",
        "name": "Expedition Logistics"
      }
    ],
    "status": "PUBLISHED",
    "publishedAt": "2026-01-10T09:00:00.000Z",
    "createdAt": "2026-01-08T09:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "body": "## Portal Launch Announcement (Demo)\n\nDemonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.\n\nPlaceholder body text for the news/event detail page.",
```

| `data.categories[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PENDING_REVIEW`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Editorial workflow status. Public endpoints only ever return PUBLISHED records. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
}
```

## 4.10 Search

Unified search and autocomplete.

<a id="op-search"></a>

### Unified search

`GET /api/v1/search`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Unified search |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/search` |
| **Operation ID** | `search` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 60 requests per 60s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Search Results (`/search`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `search()` — `@/lib/api/search` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Searches expeditions, publications, datasets, media, news/activities and published outreach content in one call. Returns relevance-ranked results plus facet counts for building filters.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | string | yes |  | length 2–200 | Search text (min 2 characters). |
| `types` | enum[] | no |  | Values: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY`, `OUTREACH`; min 1 item(s) | Comma-separated list, e.g. `types=PUBLICATION,DATASET`. Default: all types. |
| `region` | enum | no |  | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `category` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `yearFrom` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `yearTo` | integer | no |  | 1900–2100 |  |
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `10` | 1–50 | Items per page (1–50). |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/search?q=ice&types=PUBLICATION%2CDATASET&page=1&limit=10
```

#### Response `200`

Search results with facets.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "type": "DATASET",
      "id": "ds_demo_01",
      "slug": "demo-coastal-ice-elevation-points",

      "thumbnailUrl": null,

        {
          "value": "MEDIA",

        },
        {
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].type` | enum | yes | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY`, `OUTREACH` |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].snippet` | string | yes |  | Plain-text excerpt (≤ 240 chars). No HTML. |
| `data[].thumbnailUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].region` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `ANTARCTICA`, `ARCTIC`, `HIMALAYA`, `SOUTHERN_OCEAN` | Polar / cryosphere region. |
| `data[].year` | integer \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].score` | number | yes | ≥ 0 | Relevance score; higher is better. Only meaningful within one response. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |
| `meta` | object | yes |  |  |
| `meta.query` | string | yes |  |  |
| `meta.tookMs` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `meta.facets` | object | yes |  | Counts over ALL matches (ignoring pagination) for building filter UIs. |
| `meta.facets.types` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.types[].value` | string | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.types[].count` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `meta.facets.regions` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.regions[].value` | string | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.regions[].count` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `meta.facets.years` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.years[].value` | string | yes |  |  |
| `meta.facets.years[].count` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | `q` missing/too short, or another parameter is invalid. |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Search Results (`/search`)
- **When to call:** When the search page loads with `?q=` and whenever query/filters/page change.
- **Caching:** Public data; ~1 min staleness is fine. Do not persist results beyond the current view. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** None.
- Keep `q`, `types`, `region`, `page` in the URL (`/search?q=ice&types=DATASET`).
- Build links with `searchResultHref(result)` from src/lib/api/search.ts.
- Facet counts ignore pagination — use them for filter chips (e.g. “Datasets (12)”).
- Do not call with fewer than 2 characters; show a prompt instead.

```ts
import { search } from "@/lib/api/search";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await search({
    q: "ice",
    types: [
      "PUBLICATION",
      "DATASET"
    ],
    page: 1,
    limit: 10
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/search?q=ice&types=PUBLICATION%2CDATASET&page=1&limit=10`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-getsearchsuggestions"></a>

### Search autocomplete

`GET /api/v1/search/suggest`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Search autocomplete |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/search/suggest` |
| **Operation ID** | `getSearchSuggestions` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 120 requests per 60s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Global header (search box, user menu)<br>Search Results (`/search`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getSearchSuggestions()` — `@/lib/api/search` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Fast type-ahead suggestions from titles, keywords and categories.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | string | yes |  | length 1–100 |  |
| `limit` | integer | no | `8` | 1–20 |  |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/search/suggest?q=gla&limit=5
```

#### Response `200`

Suggestions.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "text": "glacier mass balance",
      "kind": "KEYWORD",
      "resultType": null,
      "slug": null
    },
    {
      "text": "Glaciology",
      "kind": "CATEGORY",
      "resultType": null,
      "slug": "glaciology"
    }
  ]
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].text` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].kind` | enum | yes | One of: `TITLE`, `KEYWORD`, `CATEGORY` |  |
| `data[].resultType` | enum \| null | yes | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY`, `OUTREACH` | For TITLE suggestions: which kind of record. |
| `data[].slug` | string \| null | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | For TITLE/CATEGORY suggestions: navigate directly. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Global header (search box, user menu), Search Results (`/search`)
- **When to call:** As the user types in the search box — debounce ~200 ms and abort the previous request (`signal`).
- **Caching:** Optional in-memory cache per query string for the session.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { getSearchSuggestions } from "@/lib/api/search";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getSearchSuggestions({
    q: "gla",
    limit: 5
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/search/suggest?q=gla&limit=5`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.11 Files

Signed-URL uploads and access-controlled downloads.

<a id="op-createupload"></a>

### Start a file upload

`POST /api/v1/uploads`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Start a file upload |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/uploads` |
| **Operation ID** | `createUpload` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `MEMBER`, `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Rate limit** | 30 requests per 3600s per user |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)<br>My Account (`/account`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createUpload()` — `@/lib/api/files` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Step 1 of 3. Validates the file metadata against the purpose limits and returns a short-lived upload ticket. Then PUT the raw bytes to `uploadUrl` (step 2) and call `POST /uploads/{id}/complete` (step 3). MEMBERs may only upload `AVATAR` files.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fileName` | string | yes |  | length 1–255; pattern `^[^/\\\0]+$` |  |
| `contentType` | string | yes |  | length 3–100 | MIME type; must be allowed for the purpose. |
| `sizeBytes` | integer | yes |  | ≥ 1 | Exact size in bytes. Checked against the purpose limit (413). |
| `purpose` | enum | yes |  | One of: `PUBLICATION_PDF`, `DATASET_FILE`, `MEDIA`, `COVER_IMAGE`, `ATTACHMENT`, `AVATAR` |  |
| `accessLevel` | enum | no |  | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | Defaults to OPEN. |
| `checksumSha256` | string | no |  | pattern `^[a-f0-9]{64}$` | Optional hex SHA-256; verified on completion. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/uploads
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fileName": "demo-figure.png",
  "contentType": "image/png",
  "sizeBytes": 482113,
  "purpose": "COVER_IMAGE"
}
```

#### Response `201`

Upload ticket.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "fileId": "file_mock_1009",
    "method": "PUT",
    "uploadUrl": "/api/v1/uploads/file_mock_1009/content",
    "headers": {
      "Content-Type": "image/png"
    },
    "expiresAt": "2026-01-15T12:15:00.000Z",
    "maxSizeBytes": 15728640
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.fileId` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.method` | `"PUT"` | yes |  |  |
| `data.uploadUrl` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Send the raw file bytes here with the given method and headers. A signed object-storage URL in production, or `/api/v1/uploads/{id}/content` with the local driver. |
| `data.headers` | object (map) | yes |  | Headers that MUST be sent with the upload request. |
| `data.expiresAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.maxSizeBytes` | integer | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Role may not upload this purpose (e.g. MEMBER uploading a dataset), or disallowed Origin. |
| 413 | `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` | `sizeBytes` exceeds the limit for this purpose (`details.maxSizeBytes`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Invalid body, or `contentType` not allowed for this purpose. |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`), My Account (`/account`)
- **When to call:** When the user picks a file in an upload field.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.
- Use the `uploadFile(file, { purpose, onProgress })` helper in src/lib/api/files.ts — it performs all three steps.
- Uploads use a signed URL / raw PUT, NOT multipart/form-data.

```ts
import { createUpload } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createUpload({
    fileName: "demo-figure.png",
    contentType: "image/png",
    sizeBytes: 482113,
    purpose: "COVER_IMAGE"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/uploads`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"fileName":"demo-figure.png","contentType":"image/png","sizeBytes":482113,"purpose":"COVER_IMAGE"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-putuploadcontent"></a>

### Upload file bytes (local storage driver)

`PUT /api/v1/uploads/{id}/content`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Upload file bytes (local storage driver) |
| **Method** | `PUT` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/uploads/{id}/content` |
| **Operation ID** | `putUploadContent` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `MEMBER`, `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `uploadFileContent()` — `@/lib/api/files` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Step 2 when `STORAGE_DRIVER=local` (development). Body is the raw file (`application/octet-stream` or the declared content type). With S3-compatible storage, `uploadUrl` points to the object store instead and this endpoint is not used.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — raw file bytes (`application/octet-stream` or the declared content type). Not multipart.

Example:

```http
PUT /api/v1/uploads/file_mock_1010/content
```

#### Response `200`

Bytes stored.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "fileId": "file_mock_1010",
    "receivedBytes": 482113
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.fileId` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.receivedBytes` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Ticket belongs to another user. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Upload ticket not found or expired. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Upload already completed. |
| 413 | `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` | Body larger than the declared `sizeBytes`. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** Called by `uploadFile()` — never construct this URL yourself; always use `uploadUrl` from the ticket.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { uploadFile } from "@/lib/api/files";

// uploadFile() performs createUpload → PUT bytes → completeUpload:
const file = await uploadFile(input.files[0], { purpose: "COVER_IMAGE", onProgress: (p) => setProgress(p) });
```

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-completeupload"></a>

### Complete a file upload

`POST /api/v1/uploads/{id}/complete`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Complete a file upload |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/uploads/{id}/complete` |
| **Operation ID** | `completeUpload` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `MEMBER`, `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)<br>My Account (`/account`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `completeUpload()` — `@/lib/api/files` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Step 3. Verifies size (and checksum if provided) and marks the file READY. Returns the file record to attach to content.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/uploads/file_mock_1011/complete
```

#### Response `200`

File is READY.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "file_mock_1011",
    "fileName": "demo-figure.png",
    "contentType": "image/png",
    "sizeBytes": 482113,
    "purpose": "COVER_IMAGE",
    "accessLevel": "OPEN",
    "status": "READY",
    "checksumSha256": null,
    "downloadUrl": "/api/v1/files/file_mock_1011/download",
    "downloadCount": 0,
    "uploadedBy": {
      "id": "usr_demo_admin",
      "name": "Demo Admin",
      "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
      "avatarUrl": null
    },
    "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.fileName` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.contentType` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.sizeBytes` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.purpose` | enum | yes | One of: `PUBLICATION_PDF`, `DATASET_FILE`, `MEDIA`, `COVER_IMAGE`, `ATTACHMENT`, `AVATAR` |  |
| `data.accessLevel` | enum | yes | One of: `OPEN`, `REGISTERED`, `RESTRICTED` | OPEN: anyone may download. REGISTERED: any logged-in user. RESTRICTED: staff/owner only (contact data custodian). |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `PENDING`, `READY`, `FAILED` | PENDING until the upload is completed. |
| `data.checksumSha256` | string \| null | yes | pattern `^[a-f0-9]{64}$` |  |
| `data.downloadUrl` | string | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.downloadCount` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.uploadedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.uploadedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.uploadedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.uploadedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.uploadedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Ticket belongs to another user. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Upload not found or expired. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Bytes not uploaded yet, or already completed. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Uploaded size or checksum does not match the ticket. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`), My Account (`/account`)
- **When to call:** Called by `uploadFile()` after the PUT succeeds.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { completeUpload } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await completeUpload("file_mock_1011");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts

  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | REGISTERED file and not logged in. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | RESTRICTED file and caller is not owner/staff. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | File not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Dataset Detail (`/datasets/[slug]`), Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** Rarely needed — detail endpoints already embed `FileRef`s.
- **Caching:** Cache only OPEN files.
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.

```ts
import { getFile } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getFile("file_demo_01");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/files/file_demo_01`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-downloadfile"></a>

### Download a file

`GET /api/v1/files/{id}/download`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Download a file |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/files/{id}/download` |
| **Operation ID** | `downloadFile` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Optional — works without login; if the session cookie is present it is used (e.g. owners/staff can see unpublished records). |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Rate limit** | 120 requests per 3600s per IP address |
| **Used by** | Dataset Detail (`/datasets/[slug]`)<br>Publication Detail (`/research/[slug]`)<br>News / Event Detail (`/news/[slug]`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getFileDownloadUrl()` — `@/lib/api/files` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Enforces the access level, increments the download counter, then responds with `302` to a short-lived signed URL (object storage) or streams the bytes directly (local driver, `200`). Errors are JSON.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/files/file_demo_01/download
```

#### Response `302`

Redirect to a signed download URL (`Location` header). Local driver: `200` with the file bytes.

| Header | Value |
|---|---|
| `Location` | Signed URL, valid ~5 minutes. |
| `Content-Disposition` | `attachment; filename="…"` (200 only) |

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | REGISTERED file and not logged in. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | RESTRICTED file and caller is not owner/staff. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | File not found or not READY. |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | Too many requests. Wait `Retry-After` seconds. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Dataset Detail (`/datasets/[slug]`), Publication Detail (`/research/[slug]`), News / Event Detail (`/news/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** Never fetch() this — render `downloadUrl` as a normal link (`<a href={file.downloadUrl} download>`).
- **Caching:** Never cache (signed URLs expire).
- **Authentication handling:** Nothing to do — works logged out; the cookie is sent automatically when present.

```ts
import { getFileDownloadUrl } from "@/lib/api/files";

// Render as a normal link — do not fetch():
<a href={getFileDownloadUrl("file_demo_01")} download>Download</a>;
```

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

<a id="op-deletefile"></a>

### Delete a file

`DELETE /api/v1/files/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Delete a file |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/files/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deleteFile` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `MEMBER`, `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `deleteFile()` — `@/lib/api/files` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Deletes a file that is not attached to published content. Owner, or ADMIN for any file.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/files/file_demo_90
```

#### Response `200`

Deleted.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "file_demo_90",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Not the owner (and not ADMIN). |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | File not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | File is attached to published content — detach it first. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** When removing an attachment.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deleteFile } from "@/lib/api/files";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deleteFile("file_demo_90");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/files/file_demo_90`, {
  method: "DELETE",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.12 Submissions

Content submission and editorial approval workflow.

<a id="op-createsubmission"></a>

### Submit content for review

`POST /api/v1/submissions`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Submit content for review |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/submissions` |
| **Operation ID** | `createSubmission` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)<br>Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createSubmission()` — `@/lib/api/submissions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Moves a DRAFT or CHANGES_REQUESTED record to PENDING_REVIEW and opens a submission for EDITOR/ADMIN review. Only the record's owner (or staff) may submit.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `contentType` | enum | yes |  | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY` | Kinds of editorial content that go through the review workflow. |
| `contentId` | string | yes |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `message` | string | no |  | max length 2000 |  |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/submissions
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "contentType": "EXPEDITION",
  "contentId": "exp_demo_09",
  "message": "Ready for review."
}
```

#### Response `201`

Submission opened.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "sub_mock_1012",
    "contentType": "EXPEDITION",
    "contentId": "exp_demo_09",
    "contentTitle": "Demo Expedition: Sea Ice Thickness Pilot (Draft)",
    "contentSlug": "demo-sea-ice-thickness-pilot",
    "status": "PENDING",
    "message": "Ready for review.",
    "submittedBy": {
      "id": "usr_demo_admin",
      "name": "Demo Admin",
      "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
      "avatarUrl": null
    },
    "submittedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "reviewedBy": null,
    "reviewedAt": null,
    "reviewComment": null,
    "history": [
      {
        "id": "rev_mock_1013",
        "action": "SUBMITTED",
        "actor": {
          "id": "usr_demo_admin",
          "name": "Demo Admin",
          "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
          "avatarUrl": null
        },
        "comment": "Ready for review.",
        "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.contentType` | enum | yes | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY` | Kinds of editorial content that go through the review workflow. |
| `data.contentId` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.contentTitle` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.contentSlug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `PENDING`, `APPROVED`, `REJECTED`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `WITHDRAWN` |  |
| `data.message` | string \| null | yes |  | Note from the submitter to reviewers. |
| `data.submittedBy` | object | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.submittedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.submittedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.submittedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.submittedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.submittedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.reviewedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.reviewedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.reviewedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.reviewedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.reviewComment` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.history` | object[] | yes |  | Oldest first. |
| `data.history[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.history[].action` | enum | yes | One of: `SUBMITTED`, `APPROVED`, `REJECTED`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `WITHDRAWN` |  |
| `data.history[].actor` | object | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.history[].actor.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.history[].actor.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.history[].actor.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.history[].actor.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.history[].comment` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.history[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Not the owner of the record, or disallowed Origin. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Content record not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Already pending review, already published, or archived (`details.reason`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`), Admin › Content editors (`/admin/<type>/new`, `/admin/<type>/[id]`)
- **When to call:** When the contributor clicks “Submit for review”.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createSubmission } from "@/lib/api/submissions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createSubmission({
    contentType: "EXPEDITION",
    contentId: "exp_demo_09",
    message: "Ready for review."
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/submissions`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"contentType":"EXPEDITION","contentId":"exp_demo_09","message":"Ready for review."}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-listsubmissions"></a>

### List submissions (review queue)

`GET /api/v1/submissions`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List submissions (review queue) |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/submissions` |
| **Operation ID** | `listSubmissions` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Review queue (`/admin/submissions`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `getSubmissions()` — `@/lib/api/submissions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** EDITOR/ADMIN see all submissions; RESEARCHERs see only their own. Newest first.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `20` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `PENDING`, `APPROVED`, `REJECTED`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `WITHDRAWN` |  |
| `contentType` | enum | no |  | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `MEDIA`, `ACTIVITY` | Kinds of editorial content that go through the review workflow. |
| `mine` | boolean | no |  |  | Only my submissions. Always true for RESEARCHER. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/submissions?status=PENDING&page=1
```

| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Review queue (`/admin/submissions`), Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On queue load; default filter `status=PENDING` for reviewers.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { getSubmissions } from "@/lib/api/submissions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getSubmissions({
    status: "PENDING",
    page: 1
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/submissions?status=PENDING&page=1`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

[↑ Back to endpoint table](#2-endpoint-summary)

### Get a submission

`GET /api/v1/submissions/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Get a submission |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/submissions/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `getSubmission` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `RESEARCHER`, `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Review queue (`/admin/submissions`)<br>Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `getSubmission()` — `@/lib/api/submissions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Submission with full review history.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |


<a id="op-reviewsubmission"></a>

### Approve, reject or request changes

`POST /api/v1/submissions/{id}/review`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Approve, reject or request changes |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/submissions/{id}/review` |
| **Operation ID** | `reviewSubmission` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Review queue (`/admin/submissions`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `reviewSubmission()` — `@/lib/api/submissions` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** APPROVE publishes the content (status PUBLISHED, `publishedAt` set). REJECT → REJECTED. REQUEST_CHANGES → CHANGES_REQUESTED (the owner can edit and resubmit). A comment is required unless approving. EDITORs cannot review their own submissions.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `decision` | enum | yes |  | One of: `APPROVE`, `REJECT`, `REQUEST_CHANGES` |  |
| `comment` | string | no |  | max length 5000 |  |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/submissions/sub_demo_01/review
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "decision": "APPROVE",
  "comment": "Looks good."
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated submission.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "sub_demo_01",
    "contentType": "PUBLICATION",
    "contentId": "pub_demo_15",

    "contentSlug": "demo-paper-15-remote-sensing-calibration",

    },

    "reviewComment": "Looks good.",
    "history": [
      {

          "id": "usr_demo_researcher",
          "name": "Demo Researcher",
          "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",

        },

        "createdAt": "2025-06-02T10:00:00.000Z"
      },

          "avatarUrl": null
        },
        "comment": "Looks good.",

    ]
  }
}
```

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |

| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Not EDITOR/ADMIN, or an EDITOR reviewing their own submission. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Submission not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Submission is not PENDING. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Review queue (`/admin/submissions`)
- **When to call:** On approve/reject/request-changes.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { reviewSubmission } from "@/lib/api/submissions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await reviewSubmission("sub_demo_01", {
    decision: "APPROVE",
    comment: "Looks good."
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/submissions/sub_demo_01/review`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"decision":"APPROVE","comment":"Looks good."}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Only the submitter may withdraw. |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Submission not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Submission is not PENDING. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Contributor workspace (`/dashboard`)
- **When to call:** On “Withdraw”.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { withdrawSubmission } from "@/lib/api/submissions";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await withdrawSubmission("sub_demo_02");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/submissions/sub_demo_02/withdraw`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>
