## 4.13 AI

AI-assisted research summaries and outreach drafts (human-reviewed).

<a id="op-generateresearchsummary"></a>

### Generate a plain-language research summary

`POST /api/v1/ai/summaries`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Generate a plain-language research summary |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/ai/summaries` |
| **Operation ID** | `generateResearchSummary` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Rate limit** | 20 requests per 3600s per user |
| **Used by** | Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `generateResearchSummary()` — `@/lib/api/ai` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Drafts a plain-language summary of a PUBLISHED publication, dataset or expedition for a chosen audience. The model is grounded only in the source record's metadata/abstract and instructed not to add facts. Saved as a DRAFT `RESEARCH_SUMMARY` that an EDITOR must review before it appears on the public page.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sourceType` | enum | yes |  | One of: `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `EXPEDITION` |  |
| `sourceId` | string | yes |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `audience` | enum | no | `"GENERAL_PUBLIC"` | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `length` | enum | no | `"MEDIUM"` | One of: `SHORT`, `MEDIUM`, `LONG` | ≈80 / 200 / 400 words. |
| `language` | string | no | `"en"` | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/ai/summaries
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "sourceType": "PUBLICATION",
  "sourceId": "pub_demo_01",
  "audience": "STUDENTS",
  "length": "SHORT"
}
```

#### Response `201`

Draft summary.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "kind": "RESEARCH_SUMMARY",
    "title": "Summary: Demo Paper 1: Placeholder Study on Sea ice variability",
    "body": "**Mock AI draft (short, students).** This placeholder text stands in for a generated plain-language summary of “Demo Paper 1: Placeholder Study on Sea ice variability”. No model was called.",
    "audience": "STUDENTS",
    "language": "en",
    "sources": [
      {
        "type": "PUBLICATION",
        "id": "pub_demo_01",
        "slug": "demo-paper-1-sea-ice-variability",
        "title": "Demo Paper 1: Placeholder Study on Sea ice variability"
      }
    ],
    "id": "ai_mock_1016",
    "slug": "summary-demo-paper-1-placeholder-study-on-sea-ice-variability-5",
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "isAIGenerated": true,
    "disclaimer": "This text was drafted with AI assistance from the linked source records and reviewed by portal staff. Refer to the original sources for authoritative scientific information.",
    "model": "mock-model",
    "generatedBy": {
      "id": "usr_demo_admin",
      "name": "Demo Admin",
      "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
      "avatarUrl": null
    },
    "reviewedBy": null,
    "reviewedAt": null,
    "publishedAt": null,
    "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "updatedAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z"
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.kind` | enum | yes | One of: `RESEARCH_SUMMARY`, `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |
| `data.title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.body` | string | yes |  | Markdown. |
| `data.audience` | enum | yes | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `data.language` | string | yes | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |
| `data.sources` | object[] | yes | min 1 item(s) | Records the content was generated from. |
| `data.sources[].type` | enum | yes | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `ACTIVITY` |  |
| `data.sources[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.sources[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.sources[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `DRAFT`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | DRAFT until a human reviewer publishes or rejects it. |
| `data.isAIGenerated` | `true` | yes |  |  |
| `data.disclaimer` | string | yes |  | Must be displayed with the content. |
| `data.model` | string | yes |  | Model that produced the draft. |
| `data.generatedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.generatedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.generatedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.generatedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.generatedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.reviewedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.reviewedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.reviewedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.reviewedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Source record not found or not PUBLISHED. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 429 | `RATE_LIMITED` | AI generation rate limit reached for your account. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |
| 503 | `SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE` | AI provider not configured or temporarily unavailable. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`)
- **When to call:** On “Generate summary”.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.
- Generation can take 10–60 s: show progress and disable the button; pass an AbortSignal so the user can cancel.
- The result is always a DRAFT. Show it in an editor for human review before publishing via `PATCH /ai/content/{id}`.

```ts
import { generateResearchSummary } from "@/lib/api/ai";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await generateResearchSummary({
    sourceType: "PUBLICATION",
    sourceId: "pub_demo_01",
    audience: "STUDENTS",
    length: "SHORT"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | VALIDATION_ERROR | RATE_LIMITED | INTERNAL_ERROR | SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/ai/summaries`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"sourceType":"PUBLICATION","sourceId":"pub_demo_01","audience":"STUDENTS","length":"SHORT"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

<a id="op-generateoutreachcontent"></a>

### Generate outreach content

`POST /api/v1/ai/outreach`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Generate outreach content |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/ai/outreach` |
| **Operation ID** | `generateOutreachContent` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Rate limit** | 20 requests per 3600s per user |
| **Used by** | Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `generateOutreachContent()` — `@/lib/api/ai` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Drafts outreach material (social post, newsletter, press release, explainer, quiz, FAQ) grounded in 1–5 PUBLISHED records. Saved as a DRAFT for human review.

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | enum | yes |  | One of: `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |
| `sources` | object[] | yes |  | min 1 item(s); max 5 items | 1–5 PUBLISHED records to ground the content in. |
| `sources[].type` | enum | yes |  | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `ACTIVITY` |  |
| `sources[].id` | string | yes |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `audience` | enum | no | `"GENERAL_PUBLIC"` | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `tone` | enum | no | `"INFORMATIVE"` | One of: `INFORMATIVE`, `ENGAGING`, `FORMAL`, `PLAYFUL` |  |
| `language` | string | no | `"en"` | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |
| `maxWords` | integer | no | `300` | 30–1500 |  |
| `instructions` | string | no |  | max length 1000 | Extra guidance for the draft. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/ai/outreach
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "kind": "EXPLAINER",
  "sources": [
    {
      "type": "EXPEDITION",
      "id": "exp_demo_01"
    }
  ],
  "audience": "STUDENTS",
  "maxWords": 250
}
```

#### Response `201`

Draft outreach content.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "kind": "EXPLAINER",
    "title": "EXPLAINER: Demo Expedition: Antarctic Coastal Ice Survey",

    "audience": "STUDENTS",

        "type": "EXPEDITION",
```

<a id="op-listaicontent"></a>

### List AI-generated content

`GET /api/v1/ai/content`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List AI-generated content |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/ai/content` |
| **Operation ID** | `listAIContent` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `getAIContentList()` — `@/lib/api/ai` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** All AI drafts and published items for review and management.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `20` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `kind` | enum | no |  | One of: `RESEARCH_SUMMARY`, `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | DRAFT until a human reviewer publishes or rejects it. |
| `sourceType` | enum | no |  | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `ACTIVITY` |  |
| `sourceId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/ai/content?status=DRAFT&page=1
```

#### Response `200`

AI content.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "ai_demo_02",
      "slug": "demo-demo-social-post-transect-data-now-online",
      "kind": "SOCIAL_POST",
      "title": "Demo Social Post: Transect Data Now Online",
      "body": "**Demo AI-assisted text.** Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.\n\nThis placeholder stands in for a social post written for general public.",
      "audience": "GENERAL_PUBLIC",
      "language": "en",
      "sources": [
        {
          "type": "DATASET",
          "id": "ds_demo_02",
          "slug": "demo-ocean-temperature-and-salinity-profiles",
          "title": "Demo Dataset: Ocean Temperature and Salinity Profiles"
        }
      ],
```

| `data[].generatedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].generatedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].generatedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].reviewedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data[].reviewedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].reviewedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].reviewedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].reviewedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].reviewedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`)
- **When to call:** On AI Studio load / filter change.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { getAIContentList } from "@/lib/api/ai";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getAIContentList({
    status: "DRAFT",
    page: 1
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/ai/content?status=DRAFT&page=1`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

| | |

<a id="op-updateaicontent"></a>

### Edit, approve or reject AI content

`PATCH /api/v1/ai/content/{id}`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Edit, approve or reject AI content |
| **Method** | `PATCH` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/ai/content/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `updateAIContent` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `updateAIContent()` — `@/lib/api/ai` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Edit the draft text and/or change status. Setting `PUBLISHED` records the reviewer and makes it public (research summaries appear on the source record's page as `aiSummary`).

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `title` | string | no |  | length 3–200 |  |
| `body` | string | no |  | length 10–20000 |  |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `DRAFT`, `PUBLISHED`, `REJECTED`, `ARCHIVED` | Set PUBLISHED to approve, REJECTED to discard. |

Example:

```http
PATCH /api/v1/ai/content/ai_demo_02
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "status": "PUBLISHED"
}
```

#### Response `200`

Updated.

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Delete AI content |
| **Method** | `DELETE` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/ai/content/{id}` |
| **Operation ID** | `deleteAIContent` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |

**Purpose.** Permanently deletes an AI item.

#### Request

**Path parameters**

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Record ID. |

Example:

```http
DELETE /api/v1/ai/content/ai_demo_04
```

#### Response `200`

Deleted.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "ai_demo_04",
    "deleted": true
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.deleted` | `true` | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › AI Studio (`/admin/ai`)
- **When to call:** After confirmation.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { deleteAIContent } from "@/lib/api/ai";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await deleteAIContent("ai_demo_04");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/ai/content/ai_demo_04`, {
  method: "DELETE",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

## 4.14 Outreach

Published, human-reviewed outreach content.

<a id="op-listoutreach"></a>

### List published outreach content

`GET /api/v1/outreach`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List published outreach content |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/outreach` |
| **Operation ID** | `listOutreach` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Learn / Outreach (`/learn`)<br>Home (`/`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getOutreachList()` — `@/lib/api/outreach` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Human-reviewed, AI-assisted explainers, quizzes, FAQs and summaries for the public Learn section.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `12` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Case-insensitive text search over title and summary fields. |
| `kind` | enum | no |  | One of: `RESEARCH_SUMMARY`, `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |
| `audience` | enum | no |  | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `language` | string | no |  | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |
| `sourceType` | enum | no |  | One of: `EXPEDITION`, `PUBLICATION`, `DATASET`, `ACTIVITY` |  |
| `sourceId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Only content generated from this record. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/outreach?audience=STUDENTS&page=1
```

#### Response `200`

Published outreach items.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "ai_demo_01",
      "slug": "demo-demo-explainer-what-happens-on-a-coastal-ice-survey",
      "kind": "EXPLAINER",
      "title": "Demo Explainer: What Happens on a Coastal Ice Survey?",
      "audience": "STUDENTS",
      "language": "en",
      "isAIGenerated": true,
      "publishedAt": "2025-06-01T08:00:00.000Z",
      "excerpt": "Demo AI assisted text. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information. This placeholder stands in for a explainer written for students."
    },
    {
      "id": "ai_demo_04",
      "slug": "demo-demo-quiz-polar-regions-basics",
      "kind": "QUIZ",
      "title": "Demo Quiz: Polar Regions Basics",
      "audience": "STUDENTS",
      "language": "en",
      "isAIGenerated": true,
      "publishedAt": "2025-06-01T08:00:00.000Z",
      "excerpt": "Demo AI assisted text. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information. This placeholder stands in for a quiz written for students."
    }
  ],
  "pagination": {
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 12,
    "total": 2,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "hasNextPage": false,
    "hasPreviousPage": false
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].kind` | enum | yes | One of: `RESEARCH_SUMMARY`, `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |
| `data[].title` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].audience` | enum | yes | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `data[].language` | string | yes | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |
| `data[].isAIGenerated` | `true` | yes |  |  |
| `data[].publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].excerpt` | string | yes |  | Plain-text excerpt (≤ 240 chars). |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Learn / Outreach (`/learn`), Home (`/`)
- **When to call:** On Learn page load and filter/page change.
- **Caching:** Public, shareable data; up to ~5 min staleness is fine. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** None.
- Show an “AI-assisted” badge on every item (`isAIGenerated` is always true).

```ts
import { getOutreachList } from "@/lib/api/outreach";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await getOutreachList({
    audience: "STUDENTS",
    page: 1
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/outreach?audience=STUDENTS&page=1`, {

  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.kind` | enum | yes | One of: `RESEARCH_SUMMARY`, `SOCIAL_POST`, `NEWSLETTER`, `PRESS_RELEASE`, `EXPLAINER`, `QUIZ`, `FAQ` |  |

| `data.body` | string | yes |  | Markdown. |
| `data.audience` | enum | yes | One of: `GENERAL_PUBLIC`, `STUDENTS`, `EDUCATORS`, `POLICY_MAKERS`, `RESEARCHERS` |  |
| `data.language` | string | yes | pattern `^[a-z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2})?$` | BCP 47 language tag, e.g. `en`, `hi`. |
| `data.sources` | object[] | yes | min 1 item(s) | Records the content was generated from. |

| `data.sources[].title` | string | yes |  |  |

| `data.disclaimer` | string | yes |  | Must be displayed with the content. |
| `data.model` | string | yes |  | Model that produced the draft. |
| `data.generatedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.generatedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.generatedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.generatedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.generatedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.reviewedBy` | object \| null | yes |  | Public-safe user reference (never includes email). |
| `data.reviewedBy.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.reviewedBy.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.reviewedBy.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.reviewedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.publishedAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.updatedAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |

- **When to call:** On detail page load.

```ts
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

## 4.15 Taxonomy

Research categories and keywords.

<a id="op-listcategories"></a>

### List research categories

`GET /api/v1/categories`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List research categories |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/categories` |
| **Operation ID** | `listCategories` |
| **Status** | ✅ IMPLEMENTED |
| **Authentication** | Not required — public endpoint. |
| **Allowed roles** | Anyone |
| **Public / protected** | Public |
| **Used by** | Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`)<br>Expedition Explorer (`/expeditions`)<br>Research Publications (`/research`)<br>Data Repository (`/datasets`)<br>Media Gallery (`/gallery`) |
| **UI owner** | Public frontend |
| **Client function** | `getCategories()` — `@/lib/api/taxonomy` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** All research categories as a flat list (use `parentId` to build the tree). Not paginated — the set is small.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `withCounts` | boolean | no | `false` |  | Include per-content-type counts. |
| `parentId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Only children of this category. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/categories?withCounts=true
```

#### Response `200`

Categories ordered by `sortOrder`.

```json

  "data": [
    {
      "id": "cat_glaciology",

      "name": "Glaciology",

      "parentId": null,
      "sortOrder": 1,
      "counts": {
        "expeditions": 2,
        "publications": 2,
        "datasets": 2,
        "media": 6,
        "activities": 0
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cat_sea_ice",
      "slug": "sea-ice",
      "name": "Sea Ice",
      "description": "Formation, extent and properties of sea ice. (Demo category.)",
      "parentId": null,
      "sortOrder": 2,
      "counts": {
        "expeditions": 0,
        "publications": 2,
        "datasets": 1,
        "media": 0,
        "activities": 0
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].description` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].parentId` | string \| null | yes | length 1–64 | Parent category ID; `null` for top-level. Categories are at most two levels deep. |
| `data[].sortOrder` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data[].counts` | object \| null | yes |  | Present when requested with `withCounts=true` (list) or always on the detail endpoint. |
| `data[].counts.expeditions` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].counts.publications` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].counts.datasets` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].counts.media` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data[].counts.activities` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`), Expedition Explorer (`/expeditions`), Research Publications (`/research`), Data Repository (`/datasets`), Media Gallery (`/gallery`)
- **When to call:** To populate category filter dropdowns and the Research Topics page.
- **Caching:** Public and rarely changes; up to ~1 h staleness is fine. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { getCategories } from "@/lib/api/taxonomy";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getCategories({
    withCounts: true
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

| `data.counts.media` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.activities` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Category not found. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Research Topics (`/topics`, `/topics/[slug]`)
- **When to call:** On a topic page load; then list content with `?category=<slug>`.
- **Caching:** Public and rarely changes; up to ~1 h staleness is fine. See §3.8.
- **Authentication handling:** None.

```ts
import { getCategory } from "@/lib/api/taxonomy";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await getCategory("glaciology");
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: NOT_FOUND | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/categories/glaciology`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

<a id="op-createcategory"></a>

### Create a research category

`POST /api/v1/categories`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a research category |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/categories` |
| **Operation ID** | `createCategory` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `EDITOR`, `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Categories & Keywords (`/admin/taxonomy`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createCategory()` — `@/lib/api/taxonomy` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Adds a category (max two levels deep).

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `slug` | string | no |  | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | Generated from `name` when omitted. |
| `description` | string \| null | no |  | max length 1000 |  |
| `parentId` | string \| null | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `sortOrder` | integer | no |  | 0–10000 |  |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/categories
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Permafrost",
  "parentId": null
}
```

#### Response `201`

Created.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "cat_mock_1007",
    "slug": "permafrost",
    "name": "Permafrost",
    "description": null,
    "parentId": null,
    "sortOrder": 11,
    "counts": {
      "expeditions": 0,
      "publications": 0,
      "datasets": 0,
      "media": 0,
      "activities": 0
    }
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  |  |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.description` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.parentId` | string \| null | yes | length 1–64 | Parent category ID; `null` for top-level. Categories are at most two levels deep. |
| `data.sortOrder` | integer | yes |  |  |
| `data.counts` | object \| null | yes |  | Present when requested with `withCounts=true` (list) or always on the detail endpoint. |
| `data.counts.expeditions` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.publications` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.datasets` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.media` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |
| `data.counts.activities` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 404 | `NOT_FOUND` | Parent category not found. |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Slug already in use, or parent is itself a child. |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Categories & Keywords (`/admin/taxonomy`)
- **When to call:** On create.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createCategory } from "@/lib/api/taxonomy";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createCategory({
    name: "Permafrost",
    parentId: null
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/categories`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"name":"Permafrost","parentId":null}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

### List keywords

`GET /api/v1/keywords`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List keywords |
| **Method** | `GET` |

| **Public / protected** | Public |

| **Client function** | `getKeywords()` — `@/lib/api/taxonomy` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Keywords ordered by usage (most used first). Use `q` for tag autocomplete.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `20` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–100 | Prefix/contains match on the label. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/keywords?q=ice&limit=10
```

#### Response `200`

Keywords.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "kw_demo_11",
      "slug": "ice-shelf",
      "label": "ice shelf",
      "usageCount": 11
    },
    {
      "id": "kw_demo_02",
      "slug": "ice-cores",
      "label": "ice cores",
      "usageCount": 3
    }
  ],
  "pagination": {
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 10,
    "total": 3,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "hasNextPage": false,
    "hasPreviousPage": false
  }
}
```

> Example generated from the demo dataset (arrays trimmed). Field shapes are exact.

|---|---|---|---|---|

| `data[].slug` | string | yes | length 1–160; pattern `^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$` | URL-safe unique slug, e.g. `antarctic-coastal-survey-2025`. |
| `data[].label` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].usageCount` | integer | yes | ≥ 0 | Number of PUBLISHED records using the keyword. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |

| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

```ts
  }
}
```

**Purpose.** Deletes a keyword and removes it from all content.

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |

Deleted.

| Status | `error.code` | When |

```ts
import { deleteKeyword } from "@/lib/api/taxonomy";

try {
```

## 4.16 Admin

Administration: users, dashboard, audit log.

<a id="op-listusers"></a>

### List users

`GET /api/v1/admin/users`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List users |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/admin/users` |
| **Operation ID** | `listUsers` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Users (`/admin/users`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `listUsers()` — `@/lib/api/admin` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Paginated user list with search and filters.

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `20` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `q` | string | no |  | length 1–200 | Matches name or email. |
| `role` | enum | no |  | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `status` | enum | no |  | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `sort` | enum | no | `"newest"` | One of: `newest`, `oldest`, `name` |  |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/admin/users?role=RESEARCHER&page=1&limit=20
```

#### Response `200`

Users.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": [
    {
      "id": "usr_demo_researcher",
      "email": "researcher@demo.oceanx.local",
      "name": "Demo Researcher",
      "role": "RESEARCHER",
      "status": "ACTIVE",
      "affiliation": "Demo Institute of Polar Research",
      "bio": "Demo Researcher is a demo account. Demonstration content for UI development only — this is not real scientific information.",
      "avatarUrl": null,
      "createdAt": "2025-01-10T09:00:00.000Z",
      "lastLoginAt": null
    }
  ],
  "pagination": {
    "page": 1,
    "limit": 20,
    "total": 1,
    "totalPages": 1,
    "hasNextPage": false,
    "hasPreviousPage": false
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object[] | yes |  |  |
| `data[].id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data[].email` | string (email) | yes |  |  |
| `data[].name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data[].role` | enum | yes | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `data[].status` | enum | yes | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `data[].affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  | Institution or organisation. |
| `data[].bio` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data[].avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data[].createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data[].lastLoginAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `pagination` | object | yes |  |  |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | A query parameter is missing, malformed or out of range. |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Users (`/admin/users`)
- **When to call:** On page load and whenever filters/page change.
- **Caching:** Never cache (admin data).
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { listUsers } from "@/lib/api/admin";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data, pagination } = await listUsers({
    role: "RESEARCHER",
    page: 1,
    limit: 20
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/admin/users?role=RESEARCHER&page=1&limit=20`, {
  method: "GET",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

<a id="op-createuser"></a>

### Create a user

`POST /api/v1/admin/users`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Create a user |
| **Method** | `POST` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/admin/users` |
| **Operation ID** | `createUser` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Users (`/admin/users`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `createUser()` — `@/lib/api/admin` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Creates an account with any role (e.g. RESEARCHER, EDITOR).

#### Request

**Body** — `application/json`

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes |  | length 2–100 |  |
| `email` | string (email) | yes |  | max length 254 | Email address (case-insensitive). |
| `role` | enum | yes |  | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `affiliation` | string | no |  | max length 200 |  |
| `temporaryPassword` | string | yes |  | length 10–128 | Share securely; the user should change it after first login. |

Example:

```http
POST /api/v1/admin/users
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "name": "Demo Researcher Two",
  "email": "researcher2@demo.oceanx.local",
  "role": "RESEARCHER",
  "temporaryPassword": "temporary-pass-123"
}
```

#### Response `201`

Created user.

```json
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "id": "usr_mock_1018",
    "email": "researcher2@demo.oceanx.local",
    "name": "Demo Researcher Two",
    "role": "RESEARCHER",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "affiliation": null,
    "bio": null,
    "avatarUrl": null,
    "createdAt": "2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z",
    "lastLoginAt": null
  }
}
```

> **Illustrative example** — this endpoint is **PLANNED**. The structure is the agreed contract and is exactly what the mock API returns; values are demo data (arrays trimmed).

<details><summary>Response fields</summary>

| Field | Type | Required | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `data` | object | yes |  | Full user record. Only returned to the user themself and to admins. |
| `data.id` | string | yes | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `data.email` | string (email) | yes |  |  |
| `data.name` | string | yes |  |  |
| `data.role` | enum | yes | One of: `ADMIN`, `EDITOR`, `RESEARCHER`, `MEMBER` | ADMIN: full access incl. users. EDITOR: manage & approve content, AI tools. RESEARCHER: submit content for review. MEMBER: registered public user (e.g. REGISTERED dataset downloads). |
| `data.status` | enum | yes | One of: `ACTIVE`, `SUSPENDED` | SUSPENDED users cannot log in. |
| `data.affiliation` | string \| null | yes |  | Institution or organisation. |
| `data.bio` | string \| null | yes |  |  |
| `data.avatarUrl` | string \| null | yes | length 1–2048; pattern `^(https?:\/\/\\|\/)` | Absolute http(s) URL or a path relative to the site origin. |
| `data.createdAt` | string (date-time) | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |
| `data.lastLoginAt` | string (date-time) \| null | yes |  | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp, e.g. `2025-11-02T09:30:00.000Z`. |

</details>

#### Errors

| Status | `error.code` | When |
|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BAD_REQUEST` | Malformed JSON body or wrong Content-Type. |
| 401 | `UNAUTHENTICATED` | Not logged in, or the session expired. |
| 403 | `FORBIDDEN` | Logged in, but your role is not allowed to do this (or the request came from a disallowed Origin). |
| 409 | `CONFLICT` | Email already in use (`details.reason = EMAIL_TAKEN`). |
| 422 | `VALIDATION_ERROR` | The request body failed validation (see `error.details.fieldErrors`). |
| 500 | `INTERNAL_ERROR` | Unexpected server error. Retry later; report the `requestId` if it persists. |

#### Frontend integration

- **Pages:** Admin › Users (`/admin/users`)
- **When to call:** On create-user form submit.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { createUser } from "@/lib/api/admin";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await createUser({
    name: "Demo Researcher Two",
    email: "researcher2@demo.oceanx.local",
    role: "RESEARCHER",
    temporaryPassword: "temporary-pass-123"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/admin/users`, {
  method: "POST",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"name":"Demo Researcher Two","email":"researcher2@demo.oceanx.local","role":"RESEARCHER","temporaryPassword":"temporary-pass-123"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

- **When to call:** On save.
- **Caching:** Never cache.
- **Authentication handling:** The browser sends the session cookie automatically. On `UNAUTHENTICATED` redirect to `/login?next=…`; on `FORBIDDEN` show a no-access message.

```ts
import { updateUser } from "@/lib/api/admin";
import { isApiRequestError } from "@/lib/api-client";

try {
  const { data } = await updateUser("usr_demo_researcher", {
    role: "EDITOR"
  });
} catch (error) {
  if (isApiRequestError(error)) {
    // error.code: BAD_REQUEST | UNAUTHENTICATED | FORBIDDEN | NOT_FOUND | CONFLICT | VALIDATION_ERROR | INTERNAL_ERROR
    // error.fieldErrors for VALIDATION_ERROR, error.requestId for bug reports
  }
}
```

<details><summary>Plain <code>fetch</code> equivalent</summary>

```ts
const res = await fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "/api/v1"}/admin/users/usr_demo_researcher`, {
  method: "PATCH",
  credentials: "include", // sends the session cookie
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  body: JSON.stringify({"role":"EDITOR"}),
});
const body = await res.json();
if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message} (requestId ${body.error.requestId})`);
console.log(body.data);
```

</details>

### Admin dashboard metrics

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | Admin dashboard metrics |

| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/admin/dashboard` |

<a id="op-listauditlogs"></a>

### List audit log entries

`GET /api/v1/admin/audit-logs`

| | |
|---|---|
| **API** | List audit log entries |
| **Method** | `GET` |
| **Endpoint** | `/api/v1/admin/audit-logs` |
| **Operation ID** | `listAuditLogs` |
| **Status** | 📝 PLANNED |
| **Authentication** | Required — `oceanx_session` cookie (log in first). |
| **Allowed roles** | `ADMIN` |
| **Public / protected** | Protected |
| **Used by** | Admin › Audit log (`/admin/audit`) |
| **UI owner** | Backend / admin UI |
| **Client function** | `listAuditLogs()` — `@/lib/api/admin` |
| **Mock** | Available (`NEXT_PUBLIC_USE_MOCK_API=true`) |

**Purpose.** Security and content audit trail (logins, role changes, publish/approve actions, deletions).

#### Request

**Query parameters**

| Name | Type | Required | Default | Validation | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `page` | integer | no | `1` | ≥ 1 | 1-based page number. |
| `limit` | integer | no | `50` | 1–100 | Items per page (1–100). |
| `actorId` | string | no |  | length 1–64 | Opaque record identifier. Do not parse or construct IDs on the client. |
| `action` | string | no |  | max length 100 | Exact action or prefix ending in `.`, e.g. `auth.`. |
| `entityType` | string | no |  | max length 50 |  |
| `from` | string (date) | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |
| `to` | string (date) | no |  |  | Calendar date `YYYY-MM-DD`. |

Example:

```http
GET /api/v1/admin/audit-logs?action=auth.&page=1
```

#### Response `200`

Audit entries, newest first.
